Result: 在 TabArena 上，20.81M 参数的分类模型总体排名第一，超越调优后的集成模型和 4 小时 AutoML 流水线；回归性能达到 1.64B 参数 TabFM 的水平，推理成本约为其 1/11。在 BCCO 和 TALENT 上分类排名第二、回归排名第一。在 ScoringBench 上，点估计和预测分布质量的均值排名均最佳，在 R²、RMSE 和 CRPS 评测中领先。

Conclusion: EXAONE Tabular 以紧凑的模型规模确立了表格基础模型的最新技术水平，在分类、点回归和概率回归任务上兼顾了强预测性能与高效设计，证明了架构中心化的上下文学习设计在表格领域的高效性与可扩展性。

Abstract: EXAONE Tabular is a compact tabular foundation model family for classification and regression via in-context learning, producing predictions without dataset-specific gradient updates. Pretrained exclusively on a synthetic structural-causal-model (SCM) prior, its central contribution is an architecture-centered redesign of tabular in-context learning. Rather than compressing features into a fixed row embedding before a separate row-level learner, EXAONE Tabular interleaves feature-axis attention within each item with support-conditioned item-axis attention within each feature at every Transformer layer, mediated by item-summary and feature-summary tokens. Across four public benchmarks, EXAONE Tabular combines strong predictive performance with high efficiency. On TabArena, its 20.81M-parameter classification model ranks first overall, surpassing tuned ensembles and 4-hour AutoML pipelines, while regression reaches the performance regime of the 1.64B-parameter TabFM at roughly 1/11 the inference cost. On BCCO and TALENT, EXAONE Tabular ranks second in classification and first in regression. On ScoringBench, it achieves the best mean rank for both point-estimation and predictive-distribution quality, leading the $R^2$, RMSE, and CRPS evaluations. Together, these results establish EXAONE Tabular as a state-of-the-art compact tabular foundation model family, combining strong predictive performance across classification, point regression, and probabilistic regression with an efficient model design.

</details>


### [300] [Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Aggregation in Semi-Supervised Federated Learning with non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25794)
*Rene Glitza,Luca Becker,Rainer Martin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出pFedMARL，一种基于多智能体强化学习（TD3）的联邦学习框架，通过服务端智能体动态调整聚合策略、客户端智能体平衡全局与局部更新，在非IID和对抗客户端场景下显著提升全局模型的鲁棒性、准确率和公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在非IID异构客户端数据分布下会收敛到次优且有偏的全局模型，现有的聚合策略缺乏自适应性，无法有效应对数据异构性和对抗性客户端带来的挑战。

Method: 提出pFedMARL框架，包含两层智能体：(1) 服务端智能体使用TD3算法动态调整各客户端的贡献权重以优化全局模型鲁棒性；(2) 客户端智能体平衡全局更新与本地个性化更新，无需预训练即可实现模型个性化。

Result: 在半监督音频频谱Transformer任务上，pFedMARL在多个非IID场景及存在对抗客户端时，匹配或超越了FedAvg、Ditto和本地训练方法，在准确率、鲁棒性和公平性方面均有提升。

Conclusion: pFedMARL通过MARL实现自适应的联邦聚合与个性化，能够有效应对数据异构和对抗攻击，适合真实世界的联邦学习部署。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables distributed training of machine learning models while preserving data privacy. However, FL struggles with heterogeneous, non-IID client data distributions, resulting in sub-optimal and biased global models. In this paper, we propose pFedMARL, a novel approach leveraging Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) with Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (TD3) to dynamically adapt aggregation strategies in FL settings. Our method employs a server-side agent adjusting client contributions to optimize global model robustness and client-side agents balancing global and local updates to personalize models effectively without pre-training. We demonstrate superior performance of pFedMARL for training a semi-supervised audio spectrogram transformer, matching or outperforming FedAvg, Ditto, and local training approaches across multiple non-IID scenarios and in the presence of adversarial clients. Our results indicate that pFedMARL actively improves accuracy, robustness, and fairness, making it suitable for real-world deployments.

</details>


### [301] [Geometry-Constrained Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks: Learning Edge Geometry via Banach Duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25807)
*K S Sesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出几何约束KAN（geometry-constrained KANs），将KAN中的边激活函数替换为基于Banach对偶映射的可学习函数，通过每个边上的标量指数p>1来控制几何性质。在50个符号回归目标上（40个AI Feynman基准+10个合成压力测试），几何约束KAN在中等NRMSE、平均排名、噪声鲁棒性和小样本场景下均匹配或超越固定基底的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KAN变体依赖固定基底（样条、多项式、傅里叶特征），这些基底在观测数据之前就施加了函数空间几何约束，缺乏适应性。作者希望让几何本身可学习，从而更好地适应不同数据分布。

Method: 引入几何约束KAN，边激活函数源自Banach对偶映射。每个边上的标量指数p>1控制响应特性：p接近1（亚欧几里得）产生类似LASSO的阈值行为，p=2恢复线性机制，更大值产生靠近原点的平坦响应。几何特性通过数据驱动学习而非预设。

Result: 在50个符号回归目标上：Banach-KAN中等NRMSE为0.030，与Chebyshev持平并优于样条；在18方程核心基准上平均排名最优（2.00）；噪声鲁棒性方面，ℓ^p-KAN随σ从0到1仅退化3.7倍（远优于未正则化样条的21.6倍）；小样本场景下Banach-KAN获胜次数最多。学习到的指数提供了可解释的、目标相关的几何排序信号。

Conclusion: 几何约束KAN表明让激活函数的函数空间几何可学习而非预设固定基底，能带来更好的性能、更强的噪声鲁棒性和更高的解释性。学习到的指数可作为数据内在几何结构的可解释信号，为KAN架构设计提供新方向。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) replace fixed activations in deep architectures with learnable univariate edge functions, making the choice of edge parametrisation central. Existing variants rely on fixed bases such as splines, polynomials, or Fourier features, which impose a function-space geometry before data are observed. We introduce geometry-constrained KANs, a family of edge activations derived from Banach duality maps in which the geometry itself is learned through a scalar exponent $p > 1$ per edge. This exponent controls the qualitative response: sub-Euclidean values produce sharp, threshold-like behaviour reminiscent of the $\ell_1$ (LASSO) geometry, $p = 2$ recovers the linear regime, and larger values produce flatter responses near the origin. Across 50 symbolic-regression targets ($40$ from the AI Feynman benchmark plus $10$ synthetic stress tests), geometry-constrained KANs match or beat every fixed-basis baseline on median NRMSE (Banach-KAN $0.030$, tying Chebyshev and improving on splines); on average rank Banach-KAN is best on the $18$-equation core ($2.00$) and statistically tied with the strongest spline on the full benchmark ($2.32$ vs. $2.34$). The clearest gains appear under measurement noise: as $σ$ grows from $0$ to $1$, $\ell^p$-KAN degrades only $3.7\times$ -- below even a cross-validated spline ($\approx 11\times$) -- while an unregularised spline degrades $21.6\times$; Banach-KAN degrades $8.8\times$, comparable to a tuned spline but far more stable than the unregularised one. Banach-KAN also takes the most per-equation wins in the small-sample regime, with fixed-basis models catching up only as the training set grows. Learned exponents provide an interpretable, relative signal: at a fixed initialisation they reveal a consistent, target-dependent geometric ordering across equation families and input dimensions.

</details>


### [302] [Canalization Before Generalization: Grokking as a Dynamical Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25813)
*Yiming Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究过参数化神经网络中的"grokking"现象，通过在训练平台期施加短时固定时长的权重衰减（WD）脉冲，发现其对后续泛化时间的影响从早期无序逐渐演变为稳定的剂量排序。这种排序在可见泛化出现前就已形成，且测试损失障碍趋于零，作者将这种组合称为"功能选择的渠道化"（canalization of function selection）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化神经网络中多个解都能拟合训练数据，但在未见样本上表现各异。Grokking现象将训练拟合与可见泛化分离，提供了一个研究训练过程中解选择如何发展的窗口。理解这一选择机制有助于揭示泛化的本质。

Method: 在三个grokking任务中，在训练平台期的不同时间点施加短时、固定时长的权重衰减（WD）脉冲，并测量这些脉冲对后续泛化时间的移动影响。通过比较早期和晚期平台期的时序变化、测试损失障碍等指标来分析功能选择的变化。

Result: （1）平台期早期的WD脉冲对泛化时间的移动是无序的，但后期形成稳定的剂量排序：更强的WD增加导致更早泛化，更强的WD减少导致更晚泛化。（2）这种排序在三个任务中均于可见泛化出现之前就已形成。（3）测试损失障碍在扰动基线与基线泛化检查点之间趋于零，但有序的时序效应仍然持续存在。

Conclusion: 训练过程中功能选择逐渐被"渠道化"：解的约束越来越紧密（损失障碍趋零），但同时保持对剂量排序的时间敏感性。这种"功能选择的渠道化"为理解grokking中泛化能力从何而来、如何形成提供了新的视角。

Abstract: For overparameterized neural networks, many solutions can fit the training data equally well while behaving very differently on unseen samples. Grokking separates training fit from visible generalization, providing a window for studying how this selection develops during training. We sweep short, fixed-duration weight-decay (WD) pulses across this plateau and measure how they shift later generalization time. Across three grokking tasks, these shifts are unordered early in the plateau but later form a stable dose ordering, with stronger WD increases leading to earlier generalization and stronger WD decreases leading to later generalization. This ordering emerges before visible generalization in all three tasks. Meanwhile, test-loss barriers between perturbed and baseline generalization checkpoints collapse toward zero while the ordered timing effects persist. We call this combination of increasingly constrained solution selection and persistent dose-ordered timing sensitivity the canalization of function selection.

</details>


### [303] [VINCENT: Validated Interaction Network for Cross-drug Explanation of Therapeutics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25841)
*Fan-Sheng Chuang,Xuchen Li,Yujing Bian,Kaixiong Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VINCENT框架，用于药物协同预测的基序对（motif-pair）可解释性分析。它在固定交互感知协同预测器上提取原子对证据，将原子聚为化学一致的基序，并通过重复局部扰动验证候选基序对，再将验证证据反馈以精化基序分配，从而满足化学一致性、扰动稳定性和预测器行为对齐三个标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物组合发现中，单一协同分数不足以支撑研究决策，研究者还需知道哪些分子区域共同驱动预测。现有可解释性模型将解释内建于预测器架构，缺乏跨药物区域分数的扰动验证，也未将验证证据反馈以精化解释。因此需要一种可靠的基序对解释方法，同时满足化学一致性、扰动稳定性和预测器行为对齐。

Method: VINCENT采用训后（post-training）框架：1) 从注意力和梯度信号中提取原子对证据；2) 将原子聚为化学一致的基序；3) 通过重复局部扰动验证候选基序对；4) 将验证证据反馈以精化基序分配，闭环迭代产生满足三标准的解释。

Result: 在25对文献注释子集上，VINCENT平均基序召回率达0.826（95% CI: 0.78-0.87），优于基线方法的0.49-0.66；在全部71对测试数据上，其验证交互分数实现了3.36的TP/TN分离度。

Conclusion: 闭环扰动验证能比现有替代方案更准确地恢复文献支持的分子区域，同时产生更能反映预测器行为的跨药物交互分数。

Abstract: Drug synergy prediction estimates whether two drugs produce a stronger joint effect than expected from their individual activities. For drug combination discovery, a single synergy score is often not enough: researchers also need to know which molecular regions jointly drive the prediction. We study motif-pair synergy explanation, which identifies pairs of chemically coherent regions, one from each drug, that jointly contribute to predicted synergy. Existing interpretable synergy models expose atom- or substructure-level signals, but their explanations are built into the predictor architecture, and none validates cross-drug region scores under repeated perturbations or feeds that evidence back to refine the explanation. A reliable motif-pair explanation should instead be chemically coherent, perturbation-stable, and aligned with predictor behavior. We introduce VINCENT (Validated Interaction Network for Cross-drug Explanation of Therapeutics), a post-training framework for a fixed interaction-aware synergy predictor. VINCENT extracts atom-pair evidence from attention and gradient signals, groups atoms into chemically coherent motifs, and validates candidate motif pairs through repeated local perturbations. The validated evidence is fed back to refine motif assignments, yielding explanations that satisfy these three criteria. On a 25-pair literature-annotated subset, VINCENT achieves a mean motif recall of 0.826 (95% CI: 0.78-0.87), compared with 0.49-0.66 for baselines. Across all 71 test pairs, its validated interaction scores yield a TP/TN separation of 3.36. These results show that closed-loop perturbation validation recovers literature-supported molecular regions more accurately than existing alternatives while producing cross-drug interaction scores that better reflect predictor behavior.

</details>


### [304] [CEDAR: Controlled and Event-Driven Demand Forecasting via Residual Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25871)
*Junjie Meng,Ranxu Zhang,Zi-an Zhang,Shujun Liu,Xiaoning Qi,Xiaozhou Xu,Yanyong Zhang,Hui Xiong,Chao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CEDAR框架，用于大规模电商市场中的可控需求模拟预测。该框架通过两阶段设计（Action-Interleaved Transformer学习动作条件下的状态迁移 + Residual Correction Module利用外部事件信号和LLM辅助文本表征修正偏差），解决了传统时间序列预测在决策条件下模拟时存在的自回归惯性和策略不敏感问题。基于阿里1688约3200万商品轨迹的真实数据验证，CEDAR在离线实验和生产在线实验中均优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间序列预测方法本质上是被动的，即使将操作决策作为辅助协变量，也仅优化历史策略下的相关性外推，导致自回归惯性和内生市场演化与决策诱导迁移的混淆，无法支持需要评估未来动作序列（如预算排期）影响的规划场景。

Method: 两阶段框架：Stage I采用Action-Interleaved Transformer学习可控的动作条件状态迁移，用于计划干预下的滚动模拟；Stage II通过Residual Correction Module利用外部事件信号和LLM辅助文本表征，对齐噪声事件描述与产品上下文，修正事件驱动的偏差。

Result: 在阿里1688约3200万产品轨迹的大规模真实数据集上，离线实验和在线受控实验均表明CEDAR在模拟准确性上持续优于强TSF基线，并为实际预算规划带来实际收益。

Conclusion: CEDAR通过将动作干预与事件修正嵌入预测框架，实现了策略敏感的决策条件模拟，为电商平台在大规模市场中的先验规划提供了可靠支撑。

Abstract: Forecasting in large-scale e-commerce marketplaces is increasingly required to support planning: merchants need to evaluate sales outcomes under future action sequences such as budget schedules, rather than passively predicting what happens next. However, most existing time series forecasting (TSF) approaches remain inherently passive. Even when incorporating operational decisions as auxiliary covariates, they typically optimize for correlation-based extrapolation under historical policies. This design suffers from autoregressive inertia and conflates endogenous market evolution with decision-induced transitions, leading to policy-insensitive rollouts and unreliable counterfactual analysis. To bridge this gap, we propose CEDAR (Controlled and Event-Driven Demand forecasting via Action-aware Residual decomposition), a two-stage framework for robust decision-conditioned simulation. In Stage I, an Action-Interleaved Transformer learns controllable action-conditioned state transitions for rollout under planned interventions. In Stage II, a Residual Correction Module leverages external event signals and LLM-assisted text representations to align noisy event descriptions with product context and correct event-driven deviations. Our study is enabled by a large-scale real-world dataset from Alibaba 1688, comprising approximately 32 million product trajectories with paired state-action sequences and aligned event signals. Extensive offline experiments and online controlled experiments in production demonstrate that CEDAR consistently improves simulation accuracy over strong TSF baselines and delivers practical gains for real-world budget planning.

</details>


### [305] [How Edge of Stability Hinders SCAFFOLD in Federated Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25873)
*Anant Khandelwal,Michael Crawshaw,Mingrui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出联邦优化中存在Edge of Stability (EoS)动态和渐进锐化现象，解释了SCAFFOLD算法理论上优于FedAvg但在实践中表现不佳的根本原因——在EoS状态下，SCAFFOLD对全局梯度的估计能力严重下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SCAFFOLD算法在理论上具有强收敛保证且不受数据异质性影响，但在实际深度学习中通常不如简单的FedAvg。作者希望揭示这一理论与实践之间的差距背后的机制。现有研究忽视了EoS和渐进锐化在联邦优化中的作用。

Method: 通过大量经验探测实验，系统性地研究FedAvg和SCAFFOLD在不同架构和超参数下的优化动态：(1)检查EoS动态是否出现；(2)测量锐度（sharpness）的平衡值与学习率及数据异质性程度的关系；(3)量化SCAFFOLD在EoS状态下估计全局梯度误差与锐度之间的相关性。

Result: (1) FedAvg和SCAFFOLD在多种架构和超参数下均表现出EoS动态；(2)锐度的平衡值与学习率成反比（与GD一致），且数据异质性程度会影响平衡值，而本地步数不影响；(3)在EoS状态下，锐度与SCAFFOLD全局梯度估计误差高度相关，表明SCAFFOLD在锐度高时无法可靠估计全局梯度。

Conclusion: SCAFFOLD在深度学习中表现不佳的机制是：高锐度出现在EoS状态下，导致SCAFFOLD无法可靠地估计全局梯度，从而削弱了其理论上克服数据异质性的优势。这为理解联邦优化算法的实际行为提供了新视角。

Abstract: In federated learning, it is well known that heterogeneous data can (in theory) slow down optimization, and much effort has been directed at designing optimization algorithms that are unaffected by data heterogeneity, such as the SCAFFOLD algorithm. Yet, despite strong theoretical guarantees, SCAFFOLD does not usually outperform the much simpler FedAvg in practice. In this work, we propose that this gap is due to the presence of Edge of Stability (EoS) and progressive sharpening in federated optimization, supported by extensive empirical probing. First, we find that EoS-like dynamics occur with both FedAvg and SCAFFOLD under a variety of architectures and hyperparameters. We observe that the equilibrium value of the sharpness is inversely proportional to the learning rate (as in GD), and interestingly, the degree of data heterogeneity (but not the number of local steps) also affects the equilibrium value. Most importantly, we observe that SCAFFOLD's ability to estimate the gradient of the global objective is severely degraded at the EoS, as measured by the correlation between sharpness and SCAFFOLD's error in estimating the global gradient along the optimization trajectory. This suggests a mechanism for SCAFFOLD's lackluster performance in deep learning: with high sharpness at the EoS, SCAFFOLD cannot reliably estimate the global gradient.

</details>


### [306] [A General-Purpose Molecular Foundation Model Transfers Across Diverse Olfactory Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25893)
*Yikun Han,Yi Wang,Neil Mankodi,Stephen Yang,Ambuj Tewari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将分子基础模型Uni-Mol2在单一嗅觉得标准基准GS-LF上进行微调，评估其是否能在多种机器嗅觉下游任务中迁移。结果表明微调模型在主基准上达到或超过现有最优嗅觉专用模型，并在跨数据集气味描述符预测、气味有无分类、对映体评估和气味混合物区分度等下游任务中均表现出良好的迁移能力。三维表示能区分镜像分子，但准确预测立体化学的感知后果仍是开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子基础模型大多针对化学性质进行预训练，但其在气味预测这一特定领域的可迁移性尚未被系统验证。作者希望探究一个经过单一嗅觉任务微调的基础模型，能否学习到可泛化到多样化机器嗅觉问题的表征。

Method: 将Uni-Mol2在GS-LF多标签气味描述符预测基准上微调，然后在四个互补的下游场景中直接评估（无额外深度学习训练）：跨数据集气味描述符预测、有气味vs无气味分类、对映体评估、气味混合物区分度。

Result: 微调后的Uni-Mol2在GS-LF主基准上达到或超过现有最优的嗅觉专用基线模型，并一致地在所有下游任务中实现迁移。对映体分析表明三维分子表示能够区分镜像分子，而二维图模型无法做到。

Conclusion: 研究支持"一次性训练、跨任务迁移"的机器嗅觉范式，表明化学预训练的分子表示可作为可迁移嗅觉预测的坚实基础。但准确预测立体化学的感知后果仍是未来需要攻克的难题。

Abstract: Foundation models have transformed molecular property prediction, yet it remains unclear whether a molecular foundation model, fine-tuned on a single canonical olfactory prediction task, can learn representations that transfer across diverse machine olfaction problems. We investigate this question by fine-tuning Uni-Mol2 on the GS-LF benchmark for multi-label odor descriptor prediction and evaluating the resulting model, without additional deep-learning training, on four complementary downstream settings: cross-dataset odor descriptor prediction, odorous-versus-odorless classification, enantiomer evaluation, and odor mixture discriminability. The fine-tuned model matches or exceeds the performance of the state-of-the-art olfaction-specific baseline on the primary GS-LF benchmark and consistently transfers across these downstream evaluations. The enantiomer analysis further shows that three-dimensional molecular representations distinguish mirror-image molecules in a way that two-dimensional graph models fundamentally cannot, although accurately predicting the perceptual consequences of stereochemistry remains an open challenge. Together, these results support a train-once, transfer-across-tasks paradigm for machine olfaction and suggest that chemically pretrained molecular representations provide a strong foundation for transferable olfactory prediction.

</details>


### [307] [Towards A Unified Information Bottleneck Framework for Time Series Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25897)
*Xu Zheng,Zichuan Liu,Zhuomin Chen,Mayur Akewar,Janki Bhimani,Jason Liu,Mo Sha,Jingchao Ni,Wei Cheng,Dongsheng Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的可解释时间序列学习框架，通过信息瓶颈原理将归因解释与反事实解释结合在一个目标函数中，引入名为{\modelname}的框架，通过参数化变换网络生成解释嵌入实例，同时产生忠实的归因和稳定的反事实解释，并在合成与真实基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列可解释方法分为两类：基于归因的解释和基于反事实的解释，但两者被独立研究。归因方法缺乏因果验证，反事实方法不稳定、易产生类对抗噪声。从信息论视角看，现有解释器易受平凡解和分布偏移的影响。

Method: 提出一个统一的目标函数，基于信息瓶颈（Information Bottleneck）原则，将归因推理与反事实推理纳入单一框架。具体构建{\modelname}框架，学习一个参数化变换网络，构造嵌入解释的实例：保留的信息产生归因解释，受控的信息移除产生稳定的反事实解释。该目标函数显式防止平凡解释和分布外反事实。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准上，与最先进的基线相比，{\modelname}在定量和定性评估中均持续优于竞争方法，产生忠实的归因和稳定的反事实解释。

Conclusion: 通过统一归因与反事实推理，并借助信息瓶颈原则规避平凡解和分布偏移问题，本文提供了一种更可靠、更稳定的时间序列深度模型解释框架。

Abstract: Explaining deep learning models operating on time series data is crucial in various applications that require transparent and interpretable insights into model behavior. {Existing explanation methods generally fall into two categories: attribution-based explanations, which identify the temporal regions most responsible for a prediction, and counterfactual explanations, which reveal how an input should be modified to alter the model's decision.} {Despite valuable insights, these two fields are largely studied independently. This disconnect leaves attribution methods lacking causal validation, while counterfactual methods suffer from severe instability, producing adversarial-like noise instead of meaningful explanations.} In this work, we revisit time-series explainability from an information-theoretic perspective and show that existing explainers are vulnerable to trivial solutions and distributional shifts. To address these limitations, we propose a unified objective function for explainable time series learning that bridges attribution and counterfactual reasoning within a single framework. Building upon the Information Bottleneck principle, our formulation explicitly prevents trivial explanations and out-of-distribution counterfactuals. {Based on this objective function, we introduce {\modelname}, a novel explanation framework that learns a parametric transformation network to construct explanation-embedded instances, where preserved information yields attribution explanations and controlled information removal produces stable counterfactual explanations.} We evaluate {\modelname} on synthetic and real-world benchmarks against state-of-the-art baselines. Extensive quantitative and qualitative results show that {\modelname} consistently outperforms competing methods, yielding faithful attributions and stable counterfactual explanations.

</details>


### [308] [Forecasting Multiple Observables with SCROLL: Score-Trained Uncertainty for Stochastic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25898)
*Pavel Prochazka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的多任务预测方法：在共享骨干网络上，为每个任务分配自由路由的最后一层信念（free-routed last-layer beliefs），通过组合各观测量的似然函数来吸收单元相关的损失缩放。在随机动力学系统（OU过程、随机Lorenz-63、真实空气质量数据）上验证，该方法在异方差系统中能分离输入依赖方差，取得最佳NLL表现并超越调参网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准多任务学习需要平衡各任务损失，通常需要调节或学习损失权重，过程繁琐且依赖人工调参。作者希望规避这种每任务损失平衡的困扰，直接通过组合似然函数来自然处理不同观测量的尺度差异。此外，随机动力学系统提供了静态基准无法提供的可计算真实预测方差，可用于严格验证方法。

Method: 在共享骨干网络上为每个任务构建自由路由的最后一层信念（free-routed last-layer beliefs），将各观测量的似然函数组合起来。单位相关的损失缩放被吸收进似然参数中，并在同一梯度步骤中学习。该方法利用随机动力学系统提供的可计算ground truth预测方差来验证模型的预测不确定性。

Result: 在良设定、同方差的OU过程上，学习到的预测法则恢复了解析核，与正确设定的基线持平。在异方差系统（随机Lorenz-63、真实空气质量数据）上，信念的输入依赖方差实现了分离：在状态和区间任务上取得最佳单次运行NLL，校准度仅被NLL更差的对照组匹敌，而计算成本仅为调参网格的一小部分。在真实序列上，状态边际在五个滚动起点上保持。

Conclusion: 该方法在良设定场景下与理论预测一致（恢复真实核），在异方差系统中展现优于传统调参多任务方法的性能，且计算代价更低。随机动力学系统作为验证平台，提供了可计算的预测方差ground truth，使理论研究与实验结果高度吻合。

Abstract: Forecasting a stochastic dynamical system rarely means a single number: one wants several observables---future state, threshold event, regime label---each with its own likelihood. Standard multi-task recipes balance per-task losses, tuned or learned. We instead compose the observables' likelihoods in per-task free-routed last-layer beliefs on a shared backbone; this absorbs unit-dependent loss scaling into likelihood parameters learned in the same gradient pass. Stochastic dynamics supply what static benchmarks cannot: computable ground truth for the predictive variance. Results land where theory puts them: on the well-specified, homoscedastic Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process the learned predictive law recovers the analytic kernel and correctly specified baselines tie. On heteroscedastic systems (stochastic Lorenz-63, real air-quality data) the belief's input-dependent variance separates: best single-run NLL on the state and regime tasks, calibration matched only by arms whose NLL it beats, at a fraction of the tuned grids' cost. On the real series the state margin holds across five rolling origins.

</details>


### [309] [One Symptom, Three Levers: A Critical Review of On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25936)
*Justin Robert,Raheel Qader*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD)方法的综述性论文，聚焦数学推理领域。OPSD通过让模型自身作为教师（利用特权信息）替代外部大模型进行蒸馏，但面临一个主要失败模式——崩溃（collapse），即模型推理路径的逐渐窄化。文章将崩溃视为由三个杠杆控制的症状：（i）信号应用位置（token加权方式），（ii）教师所见信息（特权信息性质），（iii）信号变化时机（教师动态与引导衰减）。作者不报告新实验，贡献在于提供了结构性的共享词汇体系，并厘清了已定论与仍有争议的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD方法在数学推理中展现出接近强化学习的准确率，同时大幅减少生成token数。但该方法存在一个主导性失败模式——崩溃（collapse），即推理路径多样性的逐渐丧失。作者认为这一问题是OPSD的核心瓶颈，且不同论文对同一现象使用不同命名，缺乏统一术语框架，亟需系统性梳理。

Method: 本文不进行新实验，而采用综述与结构性分析方法，将崩溃视为由三个杠杆（levers）控制的症状：（i）信号应用位置——token加权策略；（ii）教师所见信息——特权信息的类型与设计；（iii）信号变化时机——教师自身的动态更新与引导衰减机制。作者在数学推理范畴内对现有文献进行系统归类与比较。

Result: 本文提供了一个统一的术语框架来描述OPSD中的崩溃现象，明确了崩溃的三个调控杠杆及其相互作用，并划分了该领域中已被证实的结果与仍具争议的问题。文章指出特权信息是加剧崩溃的关键因素，但其并非OPSD独有。

Conclusion: OPSD是一种有前景但尚不成熟的方法。崩溃是其根本性挑战，需要从信号加权、特权信息设计和教师动态三个维度共同解决。本文呼吁研究者采用共享词汇描述现象，以促进该领域的系统进展。数学推理作为该方法的发源地，其崩溃机制研究对更广泛的应用具有启示意义。

Abstract: On-policy distillation trains a language model on its own generations while a teacher scores them token by token. It combines the dense supervision of imitation learning with the on-policy sampling of reinforcement learning. But it requires a second, larger model to act as teacher. On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) removes that cost. The teacher is the model itself, conditioned on privileged information the student will not have at test time, such as a reference solution, a plan, or environment feedback. The teacher is no stronger than the student, only better informed. Early results were promising, with accuracy comparable to reinforcement learning at a fraction of the generated tokens. But the same asymmetry that produces the signal also biases it. One failure mode now dominates the field: collapse, the progressive narrowing of the set of reasoning paths the model can produce. Collapse is not specific to OPSD, though privileged information aggravates it. This review treats collapse as a symptom governed by three levers: (i) where the signal is applied, that is, how tokens are weighted; (ii) what the teacher is shown, that is, the nature of the privileged information; and (iii) when the signal changes, that is, the teacher's dynamics and the decay of guidance. We restrict our scope to mathematical reasoning, where the method originated and where its failure modes are best documented. We report no new experiments. The contribution is structural: a shared vocabulary for phenomena named differently across papers, and a clear line between what is settled and what is still disputed.

</details>


### [310] [Quantum-Inspired Modeling of Driving Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25907)
*Mohammad Elayan,Omid Armantalab,Wissam Kontar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受量子启发的驾驶员行为表示方法，将每个驾驶员编码为演化的密度矩阵，统一表征行为的不确定性、时间演化和情境依赖的变化。该方法在I-24 MOTION数据集上无监督训练，恢复了自由流、过渡和拥堵三种可解释的驾驶模式，并能复现基本图和磁滞回线等宏观交通现象，同时支持为经典跟驰模型提供参数和为自动驾驶车辆提供实时行为预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统驾驶员行为模型预先固定行为变量及其交互方式，无法捕捉驾驶员行为的异质性、情境依赖性和时变性。灵活模型虽能捕捉这些特性但缺乏可解释性，因此需要一种既能灵活建模又能保持可解释性的统一表示框架。

Method: 采用量子力学中的密度矩阵概念表示驾驶员行为：每个驾驶员被编码为一个随时间演化的密度矩阵，该表示同时具备连续性、概率性、情境依赖性、历史依赖性，并能从数据中学习行为变量间的交互。在I-24 MOTION数据集上进行无监督训练。

Result: 框架恢复了三种可解释的驾驶模式（自由流、过渡、拥堵），捕捉了数据的行为范围和驾驶员在状态间平滑转换的过程；复现了基本图和磁滞回线等已知宏观现象；能为经典跟驰模型提供情境依赖参数，并为自动驾驶车辆提供周围驾驶员的实时行为感知和短期运动预测。

Conclusion: 该框架指向了天然可解释且值得信赖的交通模型，并发布了开源工具包以支持数据处理、训练、推理和分析全流程。

Abstract: Driver behavior is heterogeneous, context-dependent, and changes over time, and these properties shape the traffic phenomena we observe. Most models, however, fix in advance which behavioral variables interact and how. Behavior outside that form is absorbed as noise, while models flexible enough to capture it tend to lose interpretability. We introduce a quantum-inspired representation of driver behavior that combines properties usually treated separately or in part: it is continuous, probabilistic, context-dependent, history-dependent, and represents interactions among behavioral variables as learned from data. Each driver is encoded as an evolving density matrix, providing a unified representation of behavioral uncertainty, temporal evolution, and context-dependent behavioral variation. Trained without supervision on the I-24 MOTION dataset, the framework recovers three interpretable driving profiles representing three regimes: free flow, transition, and congestion. The profiles capture the behavioral range of the data and the smooth transitions drivers make between regimes as conditions change. The same representation also reproduces known macroscopic phenomena, aligning with the fundamental diagram and reproducing hysteresis loops. We also show how the representation supports practical use: it supplies context-dependent parameters to classical car-following models, and gives an autonomous vehicle a live behavioral read of the surrounding drivers with a short-horizon forecast of their motion. The framework points toward models of traffic that are interpretable and trustworthy by construction. We release an open-source toolkit on GitHub (https://github.com/mselayan/quantum-driver-representation) spanning data processing, training, inference, and analysis.

</details>


### [311] [ICON Decomposition: Multivariate Concept-Level Explanations of Deep Representations for Model Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26083)
*Roshan Prakash Rane,Marco Simnacher,Manuel Pfeuffer,Marc-Andre Schulz,Nys Tjade Siegel,Maximilian Dreyer,Frederik Pahde,Wojciech Samek,Sonja Greven,Kerstin Ritter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ICON分解方法，用于量化神经网络层中每个概念在考虑其他概念和结果后所解释的方差比例，从而更准确地识别模型真正依赖的概念（而非虚假关联），在合成数据和真实医学影像模型上优于七种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络常利用训练数据中的虚假关联（捷径学习），现有基于概念的XAI方法孤立地测试每个概念的可解码性，忽视了概念之间的相关性，可能错误地将概念相关性误认为模型实际使用该概念的证据。

Method: ICON分解方法在考虑所有其他概念和结果变量的条件下，量化每个概念单独解释的层方差比例。该方法可同时衡量：模型真正依赖的概念、未被给定概念解释的表示部分，并通过重新训练和分布外测试验证分解的稀疏性。

Result: 在具有已知真实标签的合成数据上，ICON比七种替代基线方法更准确地恢复概念重要性；在皮肤病变和脑影像模型中，ICON成功隔离了模型真正依赖的概念，量化了未被解释的表示，并产生稀疏解释。

Conclusion: ICON分解通过控制概念间相关性，提供了比孤立概念测试更可靠的概念重要性评估，为检测捷径学习和理解模型真正依赖的特征提供了更准确的工具。

Abstract: Deep neural networks often exploit spurious associations in their training data, a failure known as shortcut learning. Concept-based explainability methods screen for shortcuts by testing whether concepts such as a patient's sex or scanner settings can be decoded from a network layer. Because each concept is evaluated in isolation, these methods can mistake correlations between concepts as evidence that the model uses them. We introduce ICON decomposition, which instead quantifies how much of a layer's variance each concept explains after accounting for all other concepts and the outcome. On synthetic data with known ground truth, ICON recovers concept importance more accurately than seven alternative baseline methods. On skin-lesion and brain-imaging models, it isolates the concepts on which a model genuinely relies, quantifies the representation unexplained by any of the supplied concepts, and yields sparse explanations that we validate by retraining and out-of-distribution testing.

</details>


### [312] [When Pruning Meets Interpretability: Preserving Sparse Autoencoder Robustness in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25941)
*Suchit Gupte,Xueru Zhang,Mohammad Mahdi Khalili*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了模型剪枝对稀疏自编码器(SAE)行为的影响，理论上证明剪枝影响由扰动能量(协方差加权范数)决定，发现幅度剪枝因忽略激活几何而损害SAE功能，而Wanda和SparseGPT等激活感知方法更稳健。同时揭示中间层对剪枝更敏感的结构性脆弱点，并提出分层稀疏度分配策略，实验验证于四种模型架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE被广泛用于解读LLM内部表征，但其在后验模型压缩下的可靠性尚不明确。理解剪枝如何影响SAE行为对于确保模型可解释性工具在压缩场景下的有效性至关重要。

Method: 1) 理论分析：证明对固定SAE，剪枝影响由扰动能量(协方差加权范数)决定；2) 对比实验：评估幅度剪枝、Wanda、SparseGPT等不同方法对SAE行为的保持效果；3) 提出分层稀疏度分配策略：基于中间层更敏感的洞察，在相同平均稀疏度下优化各层稀疏度分配；4) 在四种模型架构上进行实验验证。

Result: 激活感知剪枝方法(Wanda、SparseGPT)在保持SAE行为上显著优于幅度剪枝；发现所有剪枝方法中中间层对剪枝更敏感；提出的分层稀疏度分配策略在相同平均剪枝稀疏度下实现更低的困惑度。

Conclusion: 剪枝对SAE的影响可用扰动能量统一刻画，激活几何信息是保持SAE功能的关键；中间层的结构性脆弱性提示应按层差异化分配稀疏度以优化压缩后的可解释性与模型性能。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used to interpret the internal representations of large language models (LLMs), yet their reliability under post-hoc model compression remains poorly understood. We present a systematic study of how pruning affects SAE behavior and theoretically show that, for a fixed SAE, its impact is governed by perturbation energy, a covariance-weighted norm. This perspective exposes a key limitation of magnitude pruning: by ignoring activation geometry, it distorts the learned representation space and degrades SAE functionality. Activation-aware methods such as Wanda and SparseGPT, in contrast, implicitly control perturbation energy and are therefore substantially more robust at preserving SAE behavior. We further reveal a consistent structural vulnerability across all pruning methods: middle layers are significantly more sensitive to pruning than early or late layers. Guided by this insight, we propose a layer-wise sparsity allocation strategy, achieving lower perplexity under the same average pruning sparsity. Experiments across four model architectures validate our theoretical findings. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/osu-srml/sae-robustness-under-pruning/tree/main.

</details>


### [313] [How Much Rank Does LoRA Need? Rank-Error Bounds for Transformer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26052)
*Gerard Conangla Planes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为Transformer注意力机制中的低秩适配（LoRA）更新提供了依赖任务的理论分析框架，给出了不同LoRA秩下可实现的最小期望KL误差的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA更新秩的选择通常是经验性的，缺乏理论指导。本文旨在为这一选择提供任务依赖的理论基础，通过分析注意力机制中不同秩的LoRA更新能达到的逼近误差，建立理论保证。

Method: 固定预训练注意力头、目标注意力函数和下游任务输入分布，推导秩为r的查询LoRA更新可实现的最小期望KL误差界。在目标注意力概率有界远离零时，证明误差下界正比于ψ函数；同时给出无条件上界。在可实现性、几何和矩条件下，将最佳秩r误差界定在显式倍数与尾部能量T_r的函数之间。还提供目标Fisher界、无约束下界，并扩展到多头融合LoRA和联合查询/键更新分析。

Result: 得到误差下界∝ψ(‖d‖₂)（其中ψ(t)=min{t²,t}）和无条件上界min{‖d‖₂²/4,√2‖d‖₂}。在额外条件下，误差介于c·ψ(√T_r)和min{T_r/4,√2T_r}之间。构造了softmax饱和使得匹配注意力函数所需秩严格小于匹配有限logits所需秩的显式族。多头和联合查询/键分析的扩展揭示了秩共享和因子分解约束的影响。

Conclusion: 本文为LoRA秩选择提供了严格的理论指导框架，揭示了注意力函数逼近与logits逼近之间的差异（由softmax饱和引起），并为多头部和联合查询/键更新场景提供了谱界分析。

Abstract: Choosing the rank of a low-rank adaptation (LoRA) update is usually an empirical task. In this paper, we provide a task-dependent theory of the approximation error achievable at each LoRA rank for Transformer attention. We fix a pretrained attention head, a target attention function, and a distribution over inputs from the downstream task, and bound the smallest expected Kullback--Leibler (KL) error achievable by a rank-$r$ query LoRA update. When target attention probabilities are bounded away from zero, we prove a lower bound of the error proportional to $ψ(\|d\|_2)$, where $d$ is the difference between candidate and target attention scores and $ψ(t)=\min\{t^2,t\}$. We also prove an unconditional upper bound $\min\{\|d\|_2^2/4,\sqrt2\|d\|_2\}$. Under explicit realizability, geometry, and moment conditions, we then bound the best rank-$r$ error between an explicit multiple of $ψ(\sqrt{T_r})$ and $\min\{T_r/4,\sqrt{2T_r}\}$, where $T_r$ is the downstream-weighted tail energy of the target update. We also provide target-Fisher bounds when candidate scores remain within a fixed range of the target scores, and an unrestricted lower bound when a subset of tokens carries most of the probability mass. These spectral bounds describe finite-score approximation. We then construct explicit families in which softmax saturation makes the rank required to match the attention function strictly smaller than the rank required to match the finite logits. Finally, we extend the analysis to fused multi-head LoRA and joint query/key updates, exposing the effects of rank sharing and query/key factorization constraints.

</details>


### [314] [Spectral Allocation: Why Muon Outperforms Adam, and How to Improve Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25990)
*Xiaodong Wu,Wenyi Yu,Chao Zhang,Philip Woodland*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过频谱探针分析揭示正交优化器（如Muon）优于Adam的原因，并据此提出频谱感知Muon（SAMuon）及其简化版本SAMuon-lite，通过静态频谱先验放大"主体区域"步长，在124M-1B参数模型中显著减少训练token需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon等正交优化器能大幅加速大语言模型预训练，但其工作机制尚未被充分理解。本文旨在通过频谱分析揭示其加速的底层原因，并进一步挖掘优化潜力。

Method: 1) 在真实训练轨迹上对动量缓冲区进行奇异值分解，在留出数据上估计各奇异方向的损失最优步长，构建频谱轮廓；2) 发现频谱轮廓各向异性：头部（head）波动大需小步长，主体（bulk）容忍大步长；3) 基于该发现提出SAMuon，用低秩随机SVD遵循实测轮廓，以及SAMuon-lite用两级秩一幂迭代近似；两者均不增加持久优化器状态或额外FLOPs。

Result: 频谱轮廓具有各向异性但跨批次和训练阶段稳定，且在不同优化器和模型规模下一致。SAMuon相比Muon节省13.3%-24.0%训练token达到相同验证损失，SAMuon-lite保留大部分收益且墙钟开销近乎为零，两者在所有评估的模型规模和批量大小配置中均优于tuned AdamW和Muon基线。

Conclusion: 频谱分配框架统一解释了Muon优于Adam、Adam优于SGD的原因，同时揭示了Muon均匀缩放对主体区域利用不足的局限。SAMuon系列通过静态频谱先验有效解决了这一问题，且理论上保持Muon的渐近收敛率。

Abstract: Orthogonal optimisers such as Muon can substantially accelerate large language model pretraining relative to Adam, yet the mechanism remains incompletely understood. We investigate this through an out-of-sample spectral probing analysis of Transformer loss landscapes. At checkpoints along real training trajectories, we decompose each momentum buffer into its singular directions and estimate the loss-optimal step size along each direction on held-out data. The resulting spectral profile is anisotropic yet stable across batches and training stages, and consistent across the optimisers and model scales: a volatile head operating at the Edge-of-Stability supports a much smaller step size than the tolerant bulk, which permits substantially larger steps. This profile provides a unified spectral allocation account of why Muon outperforms Adam, which outperforms SGD. It also exposes a limitation of Muon's uniform scaling: it still underutilises the bulk. Guided by this finding, we introduce Spectral-Aware Muon (SAMuon), which holds the head at the Muon scale and amplifies the bulk using a static spectral prior. We provide two variants: the complete SAMuon follows the measured profile using a low-rank randomised SVD and the simplified SAMuon-lite uses a two-level approximation via rank-one power iteration. Neither method adds persistent optimiser state or notable extra FLOPs beyond Muon at scale, and the idealised exact-whitening versions of both retain Muon's asymptotic convergence rate under standard assumptions. Across "modded-nanogpt" models from 124M to 1B parameters, both variants outperform tuned AdamW and Muon (Scion implementation) baselines in all evaluated model-scale and batch-size configurations. SAMuon requires 13.3% to 24.0% fewer training tokens to reach the same validation loss as Muon, while SAMuon-lite retains most of this gain with near-zero wall-clock overhead.

</details>


### [315] [DualOPSD: Adaptive Privileged Teachers for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26019)
*Yutong Chen,Guangfu Guo,Zhichao Xu,Kunpeng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DualOPSD，一种非对称交替框架，用于改进on-policy self-distillation（OPSD）。与OPSD固定特权教师不同，DualOPSD允许教师和学生交替更新，教师随学生分布迁移而调整，无需额外rollout。在Qwen3-8B上，DualOPSD在AIME 2024、AIME 2025和HMMT 2025上分别比OPSD提升23.61、13.89和10.00个avg@12点，同时减少截断并降低双向KL散度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD使用学生模型的特权副本提供密集监督，但特权教师在整个训练过程中保持固定。随着训练推进，学生分布和输出风格不断变化，固定的教师无法适应当前学生状态，导致监督信号与学习者错位，限制了蒸馏效果。

Method: DualOPSD采用非对称交替更新框架：学生首先从特权教师学习，随后教师在同一学生轨迹上向更新的学生分布靠拢。这种交替机制使后续监督响应学习者当前状态，且教师更新不需要额外的rollout开销。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B非思考模式下，DualOPSD相较OPSD在AIME 2024、AIME 2025和HMMT 2025上avg@12分别提升23.61、13.89和10.00点。1.7B和4B规模实验显示精度增益依赖模型规模。三个规模下DualOPSD均减少截断，4B诊断显示教师与学生间双向KL降低。

Conclusion: 动态调整特权教师使其跟随学生分布演化，能显著提升在线自蒸馏效果，且计算开销可控。但增益与模型规模相关，小型模型收益有限，暗示规模效应在自适应蒸馏中的重要性。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) uses a privileged copy of the student model to provide dense supervision without an external teacher. OPSD keeps this privileged teacher fixed, even though the student distribution and output style change during training. We propose DualOPSD, an asymmetric alternating framework that adapts both policies. The student first learns from the privileged teacher. The teacher then moves toward the updated student distribution on the same student trajectory. This update makes later supervision responsive to the learner and does not require another rollout. On Qwen3-8B in non-thinking mode, DualOPSD improves avg@12 over OPSD by 23.61, 13.89, and 10.00 points on AIME 2024, AIME 2025, and HMMT 2025. Results at 1.7B and 4B show that the accuracy gain depends on model scale. Across all three scales, DualOPSD reduces truncation. The 4B diagnostic also shows lower KL in both directions between the teacher and student.

</details>


### [316] [Robust CurveMoE: Multi-Norm Adversarial Defense for Mixture-of-Experts Models via Mode Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26043)
*Xu Zhang,Ren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Robust CurveMoE 是一个高效的混合专家框架，通过连接针对不同扰动范数特化的模型构建低损耗路径，并利用路径上模型的互补鲁棒性来同时抵御多种范数约束的攻击，在CIFAR-100和ImageNet-100上显著优于现有最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多范数对抗防御方法通常在单一参数配置下优化相互竞争的鲁棒性目标，导致巨大的训练成本和不理想的鲁棒性权衡。需要一种更高效且能利用不同范数模型互补性的防御方案。

Method: 提出Robust CurveMoE框架：(1)从鲁棒性约束的曲线位置提取干净和范数特化的专家；(2)仅对影响大的层进行选择性专家化，其余参数跨路由路径共享；(3)引入贡献引导的部分更新，利用基于初始化的梯度分数选择影响的曲线参数；(4)理论上界定了部分优化与完整曲线优化之间的目标差距。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和ImageNet-100上使用WideResNet和Vision Transformer架构，Robust CurveMoE在干净准确率、范数特定准确率和Union准确率上均一致优于MSD和ERMC基线。在CIFAR-100和ImageNet-100上分别比最强基线提升2.37和2.13个百分点。

Conclusion: Robust CurveMoE通过低损耗路径连接范数特化专家并选择性专家化关键层，实现高效的多范数对抗防御，同时部分更新策略和理论分析进一步降低了曲线构建成本，验证了该方法在多种架构和数据集上的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-norm adversarial defense aims to protect neural networks against perturbations defined by different norm constraints, but existing methods typically optimize competing robustness objectives within a single parameter configuration, leading to substantial training cost and unfavorable robustness trade-offs. We propose Robust CurveMoE, an efficient mixture-of-experts framework that connects models specialized for different perturbation norms through a low-loss path and exploits the complementary robustness profiles of models along this path. Robust CurveMoE derives clean and norm-specialized experts from robustness-constrained curve locations and selectively expertizes only influential layers, while sharing the remaining parameters across routing paths. To further reduce curve-construction cost, we introduce contribution-guided partial updating, which selects influential curve parameters using initialization-based gradient scores. We also theoretically bound the objective gap between partial and full curve optimization. Experiments on CIFAR-100 and ImageNet-100 with WideResNet and Vision Transformer architectures show that Robust CurveMoE consistently improves clean, norm-specific, and Union accuracy over MSD and ERMC. In particular, it improves Union accuracy by 2.37 and 2.13 percentage points over the strongest baseline on CIFAR-100 and ImageNet-100, respectively. Extensive ablations further validate the effectiveness of partial updating, selective expertization, and robustness-constrained expert selection.

</details>


### [317] [Group-Shared Low-Rank Approximation for Mobile-Efficient Pointwise Convolutions in Large-Kernel CNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26069)
*Hao Luo,Yiting Yang,Wenyi Zhao,Man Jiang,Zhijun Lin,Ghulam Mohiuddin,Ting Jiang,Kunming Luo,Zihao Zhang,Qingsen Yan,Guoqing Wang,Wei Dong,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通道组共享（CGS）低秩近似方法，通过SVD分解和参数共享策略，压缩大核CNN中占参数主导地位的点卷积（>87%），在保持竞争力的同时显著降低存储成本，使大核CNN模型能够在资源受限的边缘设备（如4-12GB内存智能手机）上实现可行部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大核CNN在视觉任务中表现优异，但参数呈二次方增长，阻碍了边缘部署。现有高效架构虽然通过低秩近似和权重共享压缩深度可分离卷积，但忽视了点卷积占参数主导地位（在RepLKNet-31B中超过87%），这才是资源受限设备上部署的主要瓶颈。

Method: 提出CGS（Channel Group-Shared）低秩近似方法：一种基于SVD的参数共享策略，构建与SVD分解同构的结构化低秩范式，包含跨通道组共享的降维/升维投影矩阵（高参数成本）和通道组特定的可扩展对角矩阵（低参数成本），通过组间共享实现显著的参数缩减。

Result: 实验表明，采用CGS增强的大核CNN（RepLKNet、ConvNeXt、SLaK）在竞争性性能和大幅降低存储成本之间取得了经验上有利的平衡。CGS通过缓解存储约束、降低内存带宽压力和最小化模型加载延迟，使预训练大核CNN模型能够在边缘设备上可行部署。

Conclusion: CGS成功弥合了高性能视觉模型与边缘实际部署之间的差距，证明通过针对主要参数瓶颈（点卷积）的智能参数共享策略，可以在不显著牺牲性能的前提下实现大核CNN的边缘部署。

Abstract: Large-kernel Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) deliver remarkable performance in vision tasks by significantly expanding receptive fields, yet their quadratic parameter growth critically impedes storage-efficient edge deployment. While existing efficient architectures adopt parameter-efficient depthwise separable convolution backbones that leverage techniques like low-rank approximation and weight sharing to compress depthwise convolutions, we identify a critical oversight: pointwise convolutions dominate parameter volume (>87% in models like RepLKNet-31B) and constitute the primary deployment bottleneck on resource-constrained edge devices. This results in prohibitive storage costs and severe memory-loading constraints on resource-limited devices (e.g., smartphones with 4-12 GB Random Access Memory (RAM)). To overcome this, we propose Channel Group-Shared (CGS) low-rank approximation, a novel Singular Value Decomposition (SVD)-based parameter-sharing strategy. CGS constructs a structured low-rank paradigm isomorphic to SVD decomposition, comprising shared (high-parameter-cost) down/up-projection matrices across channel groups within a layer and channel-group-specific (low-parameter-cost) scalable diagonal matrices. This group-sharing design achieves significant parameter reduction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that large-kernel CNNs (RepLKNet, ConvNeXt, SLaK) enhanced with CGS strike an empirically favorable balance between competitive performance and substantially reduced storage costs. Crucially, by alleviating storage constraints, reducing memory bandwidth pressure during loading, and minimizing model loading latency, CGS enables the feasible deployment of pre-trained large-kernel CNN models on edge devices, thereby bridging the gap between high-performance vision models and practical edge deployment.

</details>


### [318] [TraceML: An Empirical Analysis of Human-Agent Planning in Machine Learning Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26086)
*Jiarui Yan,Weiwei Sun,Sijie Li,Wenhan Li,Yiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TraceML，一个版本级别的轨迹数据集，包含4,465条人类Kaggle竞赛轨迹（134个竞赛）和430条配对的人类轨迹及207条代理轨迹。通过在每个代码版本上标注分数、时间戳、动作、意图、编辑规模和分数效果，作者揭示了LLM代理在自主机器学习开发中与人类专家的差距：人类能灵活地在数据、验证、模型和集成之间切换并重新开启已放弃的方法，而代理（Codex和MLEvolve）则陷入狭窄的循环。从人类实践中提炼的规划提示虽能改善命名行为并提升分数，但无法完全弥合差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的结果导向基准只能记录LLM代理与人类在机器学习开发竞赛中的分数差距，但无法解释其成因，因为评估只关注最终提交结果而丢弃了开发过程。作者希望理解"为什么"代理落后，而非仅仅知道"代理落后"。

Method: 作者构建TraceML数据集，将人类和两种代理（Codex和MLEvolve）在相同竞赛上的工作统一到版本级模式（version-level schema）下。每个代码版本都带有分数、时间戳，以及动作、意图、编辑大小和分数效果的标签。通过对比分析人类和代理的行为轨迹特征（如切换频率、重新开启放弃工作的倾向），并设计一个从人类实践中提取的规划提示来测试指令能否弥合差距。

Result: 发现专家行为呈现多样化：在数据工作、验证、模型更改和集成之间交替，并经常返回之前搁置的方法。而代理则陷入狭窄循环——Codex专注于重新加权集成和调整提交，MLEvolve在原地变异模型，两者都缺乏人类级别的切换频率，也不会重新打开已放弃的工作。从人类实践中提炼的简短规划提示能改善被命名的行为并提升分数，但整体努力分布仍保持"代理形态"——指令只能弥合可归结为指令的那部分差距。

Conclusion: TraceML通过公开发布包含标注的模式和提取流水线，将"开发过程"本身作为分析对象。核心结论是：LLM代理在ML开发中与人类的差距并非单一的指令遵循问题——指令只能弥合部分差距，提示本身无法改变代理的整个努力分布形态。差距中可归结为指令的部分可以通过规划提示改善，但其余部分需要更深层的改变。

Abstract: Large language models write correct code for isolated problems but remain far weaker at autonomous machine-learning development, where an agent must revise data pipelines, models, and validation over hours of feedback, and on most competitions still finishes below strong human competitors. Outcome-based benchmarks record this gap but not its cause, because they grade the final submission and discard the development process behind it. We introduce TraceML, which pairs human and agent work on the same competitions under one version-level schema: 4,465 human Kaggle trajectories across 134 competitions, seven of which are also worked by two agent scaffolds, giving 430 paired human and 207 agent trajectories. Every code version carries its score, its timestamp, and labels for the action taken, its intent, the edit size, and the score effect. Read this way, the gap becomes concrete. Experts alternate data work, validation, model changes, and ensembling, and return to approaches they had set aside. Each agent scaffold instead collapses into a narrow loop: Codex spends its steps re-weighting ensembles and tuning submissions, MLEvolve mutates its model in place, and neither pivots at the human rate nor reopens abandoned work. A short planning prompt distilled from human practice moves the behaviors it names toward the human profile and lifts scores, but the effort profile stays agent-shaped: instruction closes only the part of the gap that reduces to instructions. We release the corpus, the schema, the labelers, and the extraction pipeline at https://huggingface.co/datasets/jerryyan/TraceML.

</details>


### [319] [Agentic Autoresearch for Cell-Edge Power Control: Radically Redefining the Researcher's Role](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26093)
*Ahmad Khan,Akram Bin Sediq,Sara Azadegi Naeini,Raviraj S. Adve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文展示了一个AI编码智能体可以完全接管无线资源管理算法设计的所有层面（架构、损失函数、训练策略等），通过自研研究协议在固定预算实验中进行自动迭代改进。在小区边缘吞吐量最大化的非凸NP难问题上，该智能体在81次无人值守实验中达到收敛参考性能的99.5%，同时推理成本降低约600倍，并发现了可证明的结构性解而非调参常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无线资源管理机器学习算法的设计需要大量人工劳动（架构、损失函数、训练配方均需手工指定）。作者希望证明这一设计层可以完全交给自主智能体，从而消除人工设计瓶颈，并将问题设定在具有挑战性的非凸、非光滑、强NP难问题上以验证方法的通用性。

Method: 采用autoresearch协议：AI编码智能体编辑训练脚本、运行固定预算实验、根据单一不可变指标保留或丢弃修改。智能体拥有对架构族、输入表示、输出参数化、损失函数和任务采样律的完全控制权。通过哈希固定评估器、强制推理契约和预注册证伪器保障结果可信度。

Result: 在81次无人值守实验、26小时内，智能体达到收敛的MM参考算法性能的99.5%（单次固定成本推理），推理成本降低约600倍，从首个工作架构缩小了94%的性能差距。一个参数集适用于所有网络规模和百分位目标。发现的输出参数化在最小百分位处精确重现了最大最小最优分配，且对所有训练权重值成立。

Conclusion: AI编码智能体不仅能在无线资源管理领域实现全自动算法设计，还能发现可证明的结构性知识（如最优分配的结构）而非简单调参，表明自主算法设计可以产生具有理论价值的发现。

Abstract: Designing machine learning algorithms for wireless resource management is labour-intensive: the architecture, the loss function and the training recipe are all specified by hand. We demonstrate that this design layer can be surrendered to an autonomous agent in its entirety. We adopt the autoresearch protocol, in which an AI coding agent edits a training script, runs a fixed-budget experiment, and retains or discards the change according to a single immutable metric. We grant the agent authority over the architecture family, the input representation, the output parameterization, the loss function and the task-sampling law, and set it a target chosen for its difficulty: sum-least-percentile-rate power control across a multicell network. The formulation targets cell-edge throughput and is non-convex, non-smooth and strongly NP-hard away from its max-min vertex. Safeguards render the results trustworthy: a hash-pinned evaluator, an enforced inference contract and a pre-registered falsifier per experiment. In eighty-one unattended experiments over twenty-six hours, the agent reached $99.5\%$ of a converged minorization-maximization reference in one fixed-cost inference pass, at roughly $600\times$ lower inference cost, closing $94\%$ of the gap from its first working architecture, with one parameter set serving every network size and percentile target. It recovered provable structure rather than tuned constants: the output parameterization it discovered reproduces the exact max-min-optimal allocation at the minimum percentile, for every value of the trained weights.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [320] [Homo-RAG: Homology-Guided Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Cross-Species Gene Function Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25466)
*Azrin Sultana*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了Homo-RAG，一种基于大语言模型的基因功能预测框架，通过同源引导的多跳检索和证据感知排序，利用斑马鱼与人类直系同源关系从ZFIN、UniProt和PubMed中获取证据，在150个查询和7200篇检索文档的评估中达到NDCG@10为0.9879、MRR为0.99的性能，并为99.33%的查询检索到相关证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非模式生物的基因功能注释仍是计算生物学中的重大挑战，20-70%的已测序基因缺乏功能表征。传统基于同源性的方法成本高昂且强烈依赖高序列相似性，需要新的计算框架来解决这一问题。

Method: Homo-RAG框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 同源引导的多跳检索——利用斑马鱼与人类直系同源关系，通过混合稠密和词法检索从ZFIN、UniProt和PubMed获取证据；(2) 证据感知排序——引入证据置信度评分(ECS)，整合语义相关性、实体匹配、直系同源信息、来源可靠性和文献关联信号来优化检索证据的排序。

Result: 在150个查询和7200篇检索文档的评估中，证据加权参数λ=0.50时NDCG@10达到0.9879，MRR达到0.99，99.33%的查询能检索到相关证据。80%的检索文档是查询特有的，表明证据质量补充而非取代了检索相关性。

Conclusion: Homo-RAG为未充分研究的生物体提供了实用且稳健的、基于证据的基因功能预测框架，解决了传统注释流程的重要局限性，同时指出了证据特征和归因机制方面未来改进的机会。

Abstract: The functional annotation of genes in non-model organisms remains a significant challenge in computational biology, with 20-70% of sequenced genes lacking characterized functions. Traditional homology-based methods are often costly and strongly dependent on high sequence similarity. This study presents Homo-RAG, a framework for large language model-based gene function prediction that integrates homology-guided multi-hop retrieval with evidence-aware ranking. The framework exploits biological relationships between zebrafish and human orthologs to guide evidence acquisition from ZFIN, UniProt, and PubMed through hybrid dense and lexical retrieval. An Evidence Confidence Score (ECS) integrates semantic relevance, entity matching, orthology information, source reliability, and literature association signals to refine the ranking of retrieved evidence. Extensive evaluation across 150 queries and 7,200 retrieved documents shows that evidence weighting parameter of lambda=0.50 improves NDCG@10 to 0.9879 and MRR to 0.99, while retrieving relevant evidence for 99.33% of queries. Furthermore, 80% of the retrieved documents are query-exclusive, indicating that evidence quality complements rather than replaces retrieval relevance. These findings establish Homo-RAG as a practical and robust framework for reliable, evidence-grounded gene function prediction in understudied organisms. The study addresses important limitations of conventional annotation pipelines while identifying opportunities for future improvements in evidence features and attribution mechanisms.

</details>


### [321] [Synthesis of Hopfield Neural Network: Novel Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25481)
*Garimella Rama Murthy*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文基于文献[1]提出的将超立方体期望顶点作为稳定状态来综合Hopfield神经网络的逻辑基础，证明了无论神经元数量是偶数还是奇数，都可以将更多的超立方体顶点编程为稳定状态，为Hopfield神经网络的"编程问题"提供了新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hopfield神经网络的"编程问题"（即如何将期望的记忆模式编程为网络的稳定状态）是神经网络领域的一个关键挑战。已有研究[1]提出了通过逻辑综合方法实现这一目标，但对可编程的稳定状态数量存在限制。本文旨在突破这一限制，证明无论神经元数量是偶数还是奇数，都能编程更多的超立方体顶点作为稳定状态。

Method: 本文采用数学证明的方法，基于文献[1]中提出的Hopfield神经网络综合的逻辑基础，系统分析并证明了在神经元数量为偶数或奇数两种情况下，均可将更多超立方体角点编程为稳定状态，从而扩展了可编程稳定状态的范围。

Result: 证明了在Hopfield神经网络中，无论神经元数量是偶数还是奇数，都可以将更多的超立方体角点编程为稳定状态，突破了以往对该问题可编程状态数量的限制。

Conclusion: 该研究为Hopfield神经网络的编程问题提供了一种新的视角，表明可编程的稳定状态数量可以进一步扩大，对神经网络的记忆容量和模式存储能力具有理论和实际意义。

Abstract: Using the logical basis of synthesizing Hopfield Neural Network with desired corners of hypercube as stable states (proposed in [1]), it is proved that more corners of hypercube can be programmed as stable states (whether the number of neurons is even or odd). The research paper presents a new perspective to the so called "Programming Problem" of Hopfield Neural Network.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [322] [SWIM: Step-Wise Integrated Measure for Session-supervised List Evaluation in Generative Re-ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25104)
*Yuanhao Pu,Chenghao Zhang,Chao Feng,Xunyong Yang,Xiang Li,Yongqi Liu,Defu Lian,Kaiqiao Zhan,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出SWIM，一种用于生成器-评估器（G-E）推荐框架的列表级评估器，将用户行为建模为有限时域前缀会话级生存过程，通过递归生存分布和条件奖励分解，利用因果掩码Transformer并行估计连续概率和效用，在满足工业延迟约束的同时显著提升列表重排序效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有G-E框架中的评估器通过聚合点级价值来评分列表，隐含假设曝光独立性，无法捕捉序贯平台上重要的会话级动态（上下文依赖、用户延续性、重复内容导致的边际效用递减），导致评估不准确。

Method: 将用户行为建模为有限时域前缀会话级生存过程，将当前列表对会话级目标的贡献分解为递归生存分布和到达位置条件奖励两部分，使用因果掩码Transformer并行估计延续概率和效用。

Result: 大量实验表明SWIM在列表级重排序任务上显著优于基线方法，整体推荐参与度获得实质性提升。

Conclusion: SWIM通过会话级生存建模克服了传统评估器忽略序列平台用户连续消费行为的局限，在严格工业延迟约束下实现了更好的列表级评估与推荐效果。

Abstract: Modern industrial recommender systems have increasingly adopted the Generator-Evaluator (G-E) framework for the re-ranking stage. Within this paradigm, the generator produces candidate item lists from a pool filtered by upstream retrieval and ranking modules, while the evaluator scores these lists and selects the highest-scoring one for final exposure per request. However, on sequential platforms (e.g., short-video apps), users consume items continuously, ignoring artificial list boundaries. Conventional evaluators score lists by aggregating point-wise values, implicitly assuming exposure independence. This fails to capture critical session-level dynamics, such as contextual dependencies, user continuation, and diminishing marginal utility from repetitive content.
  To bridge this gap, we propose SWIM (Step-Wise Integrated Measure), a list-level evaluator that models user behaviors as a finite-horizon prefix session-level survival process. SWIM estimates the prefix-conditioned contribution of the current list to the session-level objective by factorizing it into a recursive survival distribution and reached-position conditional rewards. Leveraging a causally-masked Transformer, SWIM efficiently estimates continuation probabilities and utilities in parallel, satisfying strict industrial latency constraints. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SWIM significantly outperforms baselines in listwise reranking tasks, yielding substantial improvements in overall recommendation engagement.

</details>


### [323] [The "Curse of Knowledge" in LLM Query Simulation: Concept Provenance for Tracing Answer-Side Intrusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25245)
*Chenglong Ma,Xinye Wanyan,Danula Hettiachchi,Ziqi Xu,Jeffrey Chan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出"概念溯源"（concept provenance）框架，用于检测LLM生成搜索查询中的概念入侵问题——即查询包含预设答案侧文档知识，违反了搜索前用户的信息访问边界。实验覆盖77,004条查询、100个UQV100主题、8个LLM和5种提示条件，发现候选答案侧概念占7.40%，出现在97%的主题中，但仅解释不到2%的聚合评估方差，因此该框架作为边界合规诊断工具而非评估偏移预测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的搜索查询广泛用于增强IR评估，但可能包含预设答案侧文档知识的概念，违反搜索前用户的信息访问边界。现有验证指标（重叠、多样性、有效性）无法区分罕见的人类尾部变化与候选答案侧入侵。

Method: 引入概念溯源框架，将查询概念分配到四个区域：背景故事支持的、人类中心、人类尾部、候选答案侧。使用两种提取管道对77,004条查询进行跨管道验证，并通过人类验证识别人为/部署入侵机制，使用诊断探针测量局部检索效应。

Result: 跨管道token-HCIR Spearman rho为1.0；候选答案侧概念占7.40%，出现在97/100主题中，主题解释约67%方差；人类验证精确率68.2%；删除效应量d=-0.47（对比随机删除d=-0.34）；概念解释少于2%聚合评估方差。无提示条件能消除入侵，事后概念溯源选择可实现99%消除。

Conclusion: 概念溯源是有效的边界合规诊断工具而非评估偏移预测器。在测试条件下，没有任何提示条件能消除入侵，但后生成阶段的概念溯源选择能实现99%的消除效果。

Abstract: LLM-generated search queries are widely used to augment IR evaluation, yet they may contain concepts that presuppose answer-side document knowledge, violating the information-access boundary of pre-search users. Existing validation metrics, including overlap, diversity, and effectiveness, cannot distinguish rare human-tail variation from candidate answer-side intrusion. We introduce concept provenance, a framework that assigns query concepts to backstory-supported, human-central, human-tail, and candidate answer-side zones, operationalizing a boundary that retrieval metrics alone cannot detect. Applying concept provenance to 77,004 queries across 100 UQV100 topics, 8 LLMs, and 5 prompt conditions with two extraction pipelines, we obtain a cross-pipeline token-HCIR Spearman rho of 1.0 over five condition means. Candidate answer-side concepts constitute 7.40 percent of non-generic concepts and appear in 97 of 100 topics, with topic explaining approximately 67 percent of variance. Human validation yields 68.2 percent relaxed precision, revealing two mechanisms: knowledge intrusion at 45.5 percent and deployment intrusion at 45.0 percent. Diagnostic probes show disproportionate localized retrieval effects, with deletion effect size d = -0.47 compared with d = -0.34 for random deletion, but these concepts explain less than 2 percent of aggregate evaluation variance. Concept provenance therefore serves as a boundary-compliance diagnostic rather than an evaluation-shift predictor. Under the tested conditions, no prompt condition eliminates intrusion; post-generation concept-provenance selection achieves 99 percent elimination.

</details>


### [324] [Rank-Deviation Quality: A Distance-Aware Metric for Multi-Answer Retrieval and Ranking Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25318)
*Xiaokun Zhou,Alessandro Moschitti,Danielle Class*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的检索与排序系统评估指标——Rank-Deviation Quality (RDQ)，它能适应不同查询具有不同数量参考项的情况（从单一正确答案到多个有效结果）。RDQ基于有序参考列表评分候选排序，权衡检索到正确项目与保持正确顺序两方面，无需绝对相关性等级，只需序数判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的评估指标存在局限：需要绝对相关性等级（耗费标注成本），或如Kendall's tau等秩相关度量只关注排序顺序而不考虑哪些项目被返回。此外，实际应用中不同查询可能有不同数量的有效答案（从1个到多个），需要一种能够灵活适应这一情况的评估指标。

Method: RDQ将一个候选排名与有序参考列表（ORL）进行评分：每个被检索到的参考项贡献其输出位置权重乘以排名偏差惩罚，不在ORL中的项目得零分。应用特定参数控制对排序错误的容忍度，较大参数强调检索到有效参考项，较小参数更重视匹配参考顺序。输出位置权重可反映应用界面的可见性（如垂直列表或轮播图）。RDQ基于序数排名（可通过成对或列表判断获得）而非绝对相关性等级。

Result: 在包含5,000个查询、12个系统的POI数据集上，RDQ在13种评估指标配置中取得了最高的中位数经验功效@100。RDQ在200个查询时达到与其全查询排序的平均tau>=0.8一致性；最强的非RDQ配置RBP(0.9)需要250个查询才达到该阈值。在TREC深度学习基准上，RDQ在n=25时达到与NDCG相当的中位数功效，而NDCG在n=100时更高。

Conclusion: RDQ是一种高效的评估指标，在不需要绝对相关性等级的情况下，能够同时考量检索到的项目和它们的排序顺序，并且在样本效率方面优于现有指标。但在充分利用NDCG原生梯度标签的场景（如TREC DL在n=100时）仍有差距，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: We introduce Rank-Deviation Quality (RDQ), an evaluation metric for retrieval and ranking systems that adapts to queries with varying numbers of reference items, from a single correct answer to many valid results. RDQ scores a candidate ranking against an ordered reference list (ORL): each retrieved reference item contributes its output-position weight multiplied by a rank-deviation penalty, and items outside the ORL receive zero credit. Application-specific parameters control tolerance to misordering. Larger values emphasize retrieving valid reference items, whereas smaller values place more weight on matching their reference order. The output-position weights can reflect visibility in the application's interface, such as a vertical list or a carousel. Unlike metrics that require absolute relevance grades, RDQ operates on ordinal rankings, which annotators can produce through pairwise or listwise judgments. Unlike rank-correlation measures such as Kendall's tau, RDQ accounts for both which items are returned and how they are ordered. On a 5,000-query point-of-interest (POI) dataset with 12 systems, RDQ has the highest median empirical power@100 among the 13 evaluated metric configurations. It reaches mean tau >= 0.8 agreement with its own full-query ordering at 200 queries; RBP(0.9), the strongest tested non-RDQ configuration, reaches the same threshold at 250. On TREC Deep Learning benchmarks, where NDCG uses native graded labels and RDQ uses ordinal tiers derived from them, RDQ reaches comparable median power at n=25, while NDCG is higher at n=100.

</details>


### [325] [CRAMER: Control via Request-Aware Masking for Editing Recommenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25370)
*Zhiyuan Julian Su,Naihe Feng,Zhen Luther Qin,Ga Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CRAMER是一个通过请求感知掩码来编辑推荐系统的框架，将用户自然语言请求作为控制信号来调制冻结的骨干网络参数，在不进行昂贵重训练的情况下实现即时适应多种请求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的序列推荐模型虽然强大，但难以快速响应用户的即时请求。已有的适应方法要么重训练整个骨干网络，要么依赖大语言模型的推理能力（提示工程），两者计算开销都很高，限制了在大规模推荐服务中的应用。

Method: 受模型控制理论启发，CRAMER将用户请求视为控制信号，通过对冻结的骨干参数进行掩码调制来实现即时适应，避免了昂贵的重训练过程。

Result: 在多个大规模基准数据集上的实验表明，CRAMER在多个推荐指标上优于四种最先进的请求感知基线方法，同时计算开销最小；此外还展现出更强的可控性和跨领域适应性。

Conclusion: CRAMER建立了请求感知序列推荐的新范式，通过参数掩码实现了高效、可控且可跨域迁移的即时用户请求适应。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation models, while powerful, have limited flexibility in responding to immediate user requests, making it difficult to adapt their recommendations to the user's timely interests. Unfortunately, existing user request adaptation methods often incur high computational overhead due to either 1) retraining the entire backbone network or 2) leveraging the inference ability of large language models (a.k.a. prompt engineering), limiting their applicability in large-scale recommendation services. This paper presents Control via Request-Aware Masking for Editing Recommenders (CRAMER), a framework that takes users' natural-language requests to immediately change sequential recommendation models' behavior. Specifically, inspired by the model control theory, CRAMER treats user requests as control signals to modulate frozen backbone parameters through masking, achieving instant adaptation to diverse requests while avoiding costly retraining. Experiments on multiple large-scale benchmark datasets show that CRAMER outperforms four state-of-the-art request-aware baselines across multiple recommendation metrics while achieving minimal overhead. Moreover, the proposed framework exhibits enhanced controllability and cross-domain adaptability, establishing a new paradigm for request-aware sequential recommendation.

</details>


### [326] [MOTIF: Motivation-guided Topology Inference for Cold-start Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25381)
*Yurui Shi,Yuchen Miao,Ximing Hu,Zijun Wang,Chang Han*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MOTIF是一个面向冷启动多模态推荐的任务导向拓扑推理框架，通过语义动机推理、知识增强图重建、加权图对比学习和语义-结构对齐四个模块，在不向预测流程注入生成文本的前提下，学习鲁棒的图嵌入，在三个多模态基准上相较于最强基线获得最高6.07%的相对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷启动多模态推荐面临三个耦合挑战：(i)交互稀疏导致用户意图模糊，(ii)冷启动项目在拓扑上处于孤立状态，(iii)基于相似性的项目图可能引发语义漂移。这些问题使得传统方法难以有效处理冷启动场景。

Method: MOTIF框架包含四个核心组件：1) 语义动机推理（Semantic Motivation Reasoning），利用离线LLM推理推断用户/项目的动机语义；2) 知识增强图重建（Knowledge-enhanced Graph Reconstruction），重建可迁移的项目-项目拓扑结构；3) 加权图对比学习（Weighted Graph Contrastive Learning），学习鲁棒的图嵌入；4) 语义-结构对齐（Semantic-Structural Alignment），对齐语义与结构信息。关键设计是不将生成的文本注入预测流程，避免引入噪声。

Result: 在三个多模态基准数据集上的实验表明，MOTIF在基于图、多模态、冷启动和LLM增强的各类基线方法上均取得一致性优势，相较于最强近期基线获得最高6.07%的相对提升。

Conclusion: MOTIF通过语义-结构协同的方式有效缓解了冷启动多模态推荐中的三重挑战，验证了离线LLM推理与拓扑重建结合的有效性，且避免了生成文本注入预测带来的潜在风险。

Abstract: Cold-start multimodal recommendation faces three coupled challenges: (i) sparse interactions obscure user intent, (ii) cold items remain topologically isolated, and (iii) similarity-based item graphs may cause semantic drift. To address these issues, we propose MOTIF, a Motivation-guided Topology Inference framework for cold-start multimodal recommendation. MOTIF integrates Semantic Motivation Reasoning, Knowledge-enhanced Graph Reconstruction, Weighted Graph Contrastive Learning, and Semantic-Structural Alignment. It uses offline LLM reasoning to infer motivation semantics, reconstructs transferable item-item topology, and learns robust graph embeddings without injecting generated text into prediction. Experiments on three multimodal benchmarks show consistent gains over graph-based, multimodal, cold-start, and LLM-enhanced baselines, with up to 6.07% relative improvement over the strongest recent baseline.

</details>


### [327] [DocPC: Document-Level Visual Retrieval via Representative Page Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25434)
*Chengsong You,Junwei Zhou,Nan Du*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出DocPC，一种基于代表性页面组合的文档级视觉检索框架，通过选择代表性页面并组合成单一网格图像进行文档级索引，将索引图像、向量和存储减少10.1倍，端到端索引时间减少约7.7倍。同时引入多正样本对比学习与稀疏调度的列表式优化处理文档级的多正样本监督，并构建了带多正样本相关性标注的DocViRe基准。DocPC-ColQwen在DocViRe上NDCG@5达44.09，优于最强页面级基线（38.91），同时存储减少10.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉文档检索方法以页面为中心，与需要完整文档检索的真实场景不符。简单的"先页面后文档"聚合方案存在线性索引成本和跨页面相关性导致检索性能下降的问题，因此需要文档级别的检索框架。

Method: 1) 代表性页面组合（Representative Page Composition）：选择文档中的代表性页面，将它们组合成单一网格图像用于文档级索引；2) 多正样本对比学习（multi-positive contrastive learning）结合稀疏调度的列表式优化（sparsely scheduled listwise optimization），处理文档级普遍存在的多正样本监督问题。

Result: DocPC将索引图像、向量和存储减少10.1倍，端到端索引时间减少约7.7倍。DocPC-ColQwen在DocViRe基准上NDCG@5达到44.09，优于最强页面级基线（38.91），同时存储减少10.1倍。

Conclusion: DocPC证明了通过代表性页面组合进行文档级检索是可行且高效的，在显著降低存储和索引开销的同时，提升了文档级视觉检索性能。代码和数据均已公开。

Abstract: Visual document retrieval has advanced by encoding page screenshots with vision-language models, bypassing OCR pipelines. However, existing methods remain page-centric, misaligned with real-world scenarios requiring complete document retrieval. A naive page-then-document aggregation suffers from linear indexing cost and degraded retrieval when relevance spans multiple pages. We propose DocPC, a document-level visual retrieval framework based on Representative Page Composition: selecting representative pages and composing them into a single grid image for document-level indexing, reducing indexed images, vectors, and storage by 10.1x and end-to-end indexing time by roughly 7.7x. To handle multi-positive supervision prevalent at the document level, we combine multi-positive contrastive learning with sparsely scheduled listwise optimization. We also introduce DocViRe, a benchmark with multi-positive relevance annotations. DocPC-ColQwen achieves NDCG@5 of 44.09 on DocViRe, outperforming the strongest page-level baseline at 38.91 while reducing storage by 10.1x. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DocPC-Document-Level-Visual-Retrieval-via-Representative-Page-Composition-1D52. Data is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/anonymous-7219/docpc.

</details>


### [328] [D3ER: Supporting Multi-Modal Recommendation via Disentangle and Distillation-based Dynamic Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25737)
*Bingnan Wang,Yi Li,Xiongxin Tang,Fanjiang Xu,Jiangmeng Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出D3ER方法，首次将梯度提升引入多模态推荐领域，通过交替学习模态同质判别信息(HOI)和模态异质判别信息(HEI)，并结合知识蒸馏和全局校正正则化，解决两类信息联合学习时相互削弱的问题，在真实数据集上验证了优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在联合学习模态同质性判别信息(HOI)和模态异质性判别信息(HEI)时，两者会相互削弱各自的个体有效性，这是一个核心限制。

Method: 1) 首次将梯度提升引入多模态推荐，形式化交替学习HOI和HEI的优化目标；2) 使各类型信息的专属模型聚焦于各自擅长的样本，促进专业化优化；3) 引入知识蒸馏缓解高存储成本；4) 引入全局校正正则化缓解局部最优风险。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，D3ER方法在多模态推荐任务上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过解耦与蒸馏驱动的动态集成框架，有效解决了HOI和HEI联合学习时有效性被削弱的问题，为多模态推荐提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Incorporating items' information shared among multiple modalities into a fused representation, multi-modal recommendation (MR) has demonstrated documented success than canonical unimodal recommendation. Although several attempts have been made to extract the discriminative information unique in each modality, existing methods suffer from a core limitation: the joint learning of modal-homogeneity discriminative information (HOI) and modal-heterogeneity discriminative information (HEI) tends to weaken their individual effectiveness. To remedy this deficiency, we propose a novel method, dubbed Disentangle and Distillation-based Dynamic Ensemble for multi-modal Recommendation (D3ER). We introduce gradient boosting into MR for the first time to formalize the optimization objective for alternately learning HOI and HEI. This design enables models dedicated to each type of information to focus on their proficient samples, thereby promoting specialized optimization. Furthermore, to mitigate the inherent high storage cost and risk of local optima in gradient boosting, we enhance our framework with knowledge distillation and a global correction regularization. Experiments on prevalent real-world datasets confirm the superiority of our proposed method on MR.

</details>


### [329] [Query Expansion Is More Than Generation: Improving Dense Retrieval through Better Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25521)
*Siyuan Sun,Mihai Surdeanu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出AnchorQE，一种无需训练的查询扩展集成方法，通过分别编码原始查询和扩展文本并在嵌入层面进行插值，解决了LLM生成的查询扩展在冻结稠密检索器中反而降低性能的问题。插值因子通过无监督在线策略在未标记测试流的小部分数据上估计，与广泛使用的扩展或文本级拼接基线相比，检索效果提升最高达12.89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型无需任务特定训练即可生成查询扩展，但这些扩展在冻结的稠密检索器中往往使性能变差。已有工作大多关注生成什么文本，而对如何将生成文本集成到稠密检索器中这一因素缺乏系统研究。作者通过固定生成的扩展，证明了性能下降往往可归因于集成方法本身。

Method: AnchorQE是一种训练免费的方法：将原始查询和扩展分别编码，然后在嵌入空间中进行插值融合。插值因子通过无监督在线策略估计，该策略在小部分未标记测试流上运行，其直觉是只有当扩展既检索能力强又与原始查询的检索证据一致时才赋予高信任度。

Result: 在TREC-DL、LoTTE和BEIR基准上，与广泛使用的仅扩展或文本级拼接基线相比，AnchorQE将检索效果提升最高达12.89%。此外，在线估计插值因子的策略比在开发集上调优的固定权重高出最多3.81%。

Conclusion: 查询扩展的集成方式（而非仅生成内容）是影响稠密检索性能的关键因素。通过嵌入级别的分离编码与插值，结合基于检索一致性和强度的无监督在线权重估计，可以显著改善LLM查询扩展在冻结稠密检索器中的表现。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate query expansions without task-specific training, yet the same expansions often make a frozen dense retriever worse. We identify an underexplored factor: prior work has often focused on what text is generated, while how generated text is incorporated into dense retrievers has received less systematic attention. By holding generated expansions fixed, we show that performance degradation can often be attributed to the integration method itself. We introduce AnchorQE, a training-free method that separately encodes the original query and its expansion before interpolating them. The interpolation factor is estimated using an unsupervised online strategy that operates over a small part of the unlabeled test stream. Intuitively, our strategy assigns high expansion trust only when expansions are both retrieval-strong and consistent with the original query's retrieved evidence. We show that AnchorQE improves retrieval effectiveness by up to 12.89% when compared to widely-used expansion-only or text-level concatenation baselines across TREC-DL, LoTTE, and BEIR. Further, we show that our online strategy to estimate the interpolation factor outperforms a fixed weight tuned on a development partition by up to 3.81%.

</details>


### [330] [TransRetrieval: Scaling Up Transformer-Based Retrieval for Industrial Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25528)
*Zhifei Zheng,Yunfei Liu,Bin Liu,Qiren Zhu,Hanbing Liu,Ziru Xu,Han Zhu,Jian Xu,Qi Qi,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TransRetrieval是一个基于Transformer的检索框架，通过加权平均聚合、目标token压缩和位置风格域嵌入三大技术，解决了推荐检索中特征异构导致的缩放定律失效问题，实现了计算预算和跨域数据双维度的有效扩展，在工业数据和在线A/B测试中均取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐检索中的特征异构性（heterogeneous fields）导致token范数发散，使得简单地堆叠Transformer层无法获得预期的缩放收益，即缩放定律失效，因此需要设计能恢复同质token假设的Transformer检索架构。

Method: 三个关键组件：(1)加权平均聚合（weighted average aggregation）恢复Transformer依赖的同质token假设；(2)目标token压缩，将每个候选的计算量削减85%同时保留交叉注意力的表达能力；(3)位置风格域嵌入，以极低额外成本统一多个域，将跨域数据转化为缩放资产。

Result: 在400亿交互规模的工业数据集和公开KuaiRand基准上，将每目标计算量从0.1扩展到2 MFLOPs时，Recall@2000分别提升+19.3和+22.2个百分点，确认了稳健的对数线性缩放；在线A/B测试中，在端到端延迟与生产基线相同的情况下，平台收入提升2.53%。

Conclusion: TransRetrieval证明了通过解决特征异构问题，Transformer检索模型可以实现计算预算和跨域数据的有效缩放，在工业环境中获得显著的在线收益，为推荐检索领域应用缩放定律提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Applying scaling laws to recommendation retrieval is hindered by feature heterogeneity: naively stacking Transformer layers yields diminishing returns because heterogeneous fields produce severe token-norm divergence. We present TransRetrieval, a Transformer-based retrieval framework that scales with both computational budget and cross-domain data. The key enabler is (1) weighted average aggregation, which restores the homogeneous-token assumption Transformers rely on. Building on this, we introduce (2) target token compression that cuts per-candidate FLOPs by 85% while preserving cross-attention expressiveness, and (3) position-style domain embeddings that unify multiple domains at negligible additional cost, turning cross-domain data into a scaling asset. On a 40-billion-interaction industrial dataset and the public KuaiRand benchmark, scaling compute from 0.1 to 2 MFLOPs per target yields +19.3/+22.2 pt Recall@2000, confirming robust log-linear scaling. In online A/B tests, TransRetrieval lifts platform revenue by 2.53% under the same end-to-end latency constraint as the production baseline.

</details>


### [331] [An Event is Worth One Token: Event Tokenization for Industrial-scale LLM Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25546)
*Fan Xia,Zhaoheng Zheng,Iman Setayesh,Ruogu Lin,Yiqin Pan,Samarth Mittal,Wentao Bao,Vinti Pandey,Sachin Patil,Jianpeng Cheng,Jun Xiao,Zhuang Wang,Xiangjun Fan,Sri Reddy,Minghai Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出事件中心范式(event-centric paradigm)与快照分辨率(snapshot resolution)这一新扩展维度，并引入AMBER模型，通过瓶颈事件表示(Bottlenecked Event Representation)将每个交互的完整时间快照压缩为紧凑的Event Token，作为新的大语言模型输入模态，在工业级推荐排序与检索任务上取得了计算质量帕累托前沿的进步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推荐系统每个位置仅编码文本、语义ID或少量类别特征，丢弃了事件中丰富的用户、物品、上下文和结果信号。在自回归建模下，弱查询会在序列中逐位置累积放大，导致性能退化。因此需要一种能编码每个交互完整时间快照的新范式。

Method: AMBER将每个时间快照通过瓶颈编码器压缩为紧凑的Event Token，作为LLM的新输入模态；表示端到端学习，但Event Token可预计算并缓存用于推理，从而将快照分辨率与实时推理计算解耦。论文还探索了统一的跨实体类型分词器。

Result: 在工业规模排序和检索基准上，AMBER超越替代推荐范式的计算质量帕累托前沿；在足够容量下，统一分词器优于专用逐实体分词器，展现正迁移效果；Event Token可迁移到非LLM排序器作为历史特征，带来统计显著改进，且进一步扩展分词器容量仍可提升性能。

Conclusion: 事件中心范式与快照分辨率扩展维度是有效的，通过预计算与缓存解耦策略，可以在不增加实时推理开销的情况下大幅提升推荐质量，且事件表示具有良好的跨架构可迁移性。

Abstract: LLM-based recommendation has scaled along model capacity and sequence length, yet each position encodes only text, semantic IDs, or a few categorical features, discarding rich user, item, context, and outcome signals available at each event. Under autoregressive modeling, this yields weak queries at each position and, since each position becomes context for the next, the degradation compounds across the sequence. We propose an event-centric paradigm that represents each interaction by its full temporal snapshot, and identify a new scaling dimension we term snapshot resolution: the amount of information encoded per event. To efficiently scale snapshot resolution, we introduce AMBER (Autoregressive Modeling via Bottlenecked Event Representation), which compresses each temporal snapshot into a compact Event Token, a new LLM input modality. The representation is learned end-to-end, while Event Tokens are pre-computed and cached for serving, decoupling snapshot resolution from real-time serving compute. On industrial-scale ranking and retrieval benchmarks, AMBER advances the compute-quality Pareto frontier relative to alternative recommendation paradigms. At sufficient capacity, a single unified tokenizer even outperforms dedicated per-entity tokenizers, demonstrating positive transfer across structurally different entity types. AMBER's Event Tokens also transfer across model architectures: when integrated into a heavily optimized non-LLM ranker as serving-time historical features, they yield statistically significant improvements. Further scaling Event Tokenizer capacity provides additional improvements.

</details>


### [332] [When Stale Constraints Go Unchecked: Budgeted Verification Failures in Inherited Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25553)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究智能体在继承记忆后，如何在有限验证预算下检测记录是否已被废止（supersession）。实验表明，原生分配下，过时约束导致约75%的决策与现行记录不一致；若将验证预算中的一个槽位重新分配到关键溯源路径上，一致性决策提升约61-74个百分点，且当记录与记忆一致时无副作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体继承的巩固记忆可能包含已被更新权威记录废止的约束。在稀缺验证预算下，智能体能否发现废止记录？若不能，错误能否在不增加预算的情况下避免？现有研究缺乏对"溯源性废止"和验证预算分配的显式建模。

Method: 显式建模历史溯源（provenance）不可变、但"现行记录"可变的状态；在固定预算（两条记录）下比较三种分配策略：智能体自身分配、将一槽分配给关键溯源路径、分配给随机记录。设计主运行、改写复现、跨域验证及外部沉积的稳健性复现。

Result: 原生分配在三种场景下产生77.3%、74.7%、74.7%的过时一致决策；将一槽重分配至关键路径后，分别提升+74.0、+72.7、+61.3个百分点，六个模型全部正向，且记录与记忆一致时结果不变。原始held-out场景存在时间不一致性，修正后稳健复现为+73.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 验证预算分配是过时记忆错误的主要来源，干预效果接近结构性上限。记忆系统可能需要将"新鲜度/废止信号"与"相关性"分离处理，而非仅依赖内容相关性。

Abstract: An agent that inherits a consolidated memory may inherit a constraint that was true when written and has since been withdrawn by a newer authoritative record. Under a scarce verification budget, does the agent recover the withdrawal, and if not, is the error avoidable without spending more? We model supersession explicitly -- historical provenance is immutable; what changes is which record is current -- and assign by design the memory's form, the world's state (source current or superseded), and the verification policy at a fixed budget of two records: the agent's own allocation, or the same budget with one slot re-assigned to the critical provenance path or to a random record. With a constraint stated, agents inspected its provenance path in about one episode in five; when that constraint had been superseded, native allocation produced stale-consistent decisions in 77.3%, 74.7% and 74.7% of episodes across a primary run, a fresh-wording replication and a held-out domain. Re-assigning one slot to the critical path raised current-record-consistent decisions by +74.0, +72.7 and +61.3 points, positive in six of six models in each of those runs, and changed nothing when the record agreed with the memory. The held-out scenario was later found to contain a temporal inconsistency; a robustness replication with one sentence corrected, deposited externally before execution, gave +73.3 points and is reported alongside the original. The intervention uses knowledge of the critical path and is not a scheduler; it identifies that the share of stale-memory error attributable to verification allocation is close to its structural ceiling. Memory systems may need freshness or supersession signals separate from relevance.

</details>


### [333] [RetrievalRouter: Joint Modality and Architecture Selection for Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25625)
*Emre Kuru,Mehmet Onur Keskin,Reza Farahbakhsh,Noel Crespi*

Main category: cs.IR
