TL;DR: 本文提出RetrievalRouter，一个轻量级的查询感知路由器，能够根据查询文本自动选择最合适的检索流水线（如密集检索或延迟交互检索），从而打破传统检索中"效果与速度不可兼得"的权衡。该路由器仅凭查询文本即可学习分配策略，通过单一可调参数暴露完整的准确率-延迟前沿，相比最佳静态基线在准确率上提升2.5%、速度上快12.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索流水线在模态（文本或多模态）和架构（密集或延迟交互）上的选择构成了硬性权衡：最有效的流水线运行慢且昂贵，最快的流水线却无法从复杂文档中检索出证据。实践者不得不在"遗漏证据"和"不可用的延迟"之间选择，且缺乏在查询级别自适应调整这一选择的原理性依据。作者认为并非每个查询都需要相同的流水线，这种权衡是不必要的。

Method: RetrievalRouter是一种轻量级、查询感知的路由器，仅从查询文本中学习哪种检索流水线最适合每个查询。它通过单一可调参数来暴露完整的准确率-延迟前沿，使得用户可以根据需求在准确率和延迟之间灵活权衡。

Result: 在金融和科学语料库等多个基准上，没有单一静态流水线占据主导地位。相比最佳静态基线，RetrievalRouter在准确率上提升2.5%，速度提升12.4倍。与先前自适应策略选择方法相比，RetrievalRouter在面向准确率的设置中获得了显著更高的nDCG@5，在面向延迟的设置中则在nDCG@5和延迟上均匹配或数值上优于它们。

Conclusion: RetrievalRouter证明了查询级别的自适应流水线选择可以打破传统检索中准确率与延迟的硬性权衡，为实践者提供了根据每个查询需求灵活调整检索策略的实用方案，同时兼具准确率和速度优势。

Abstract: Document retrieval increasingly supports high-stakes information access in finance, healthcare, and law. Modern retrieval pipelines vary both in modality (text or multimodal) and in retrieval architecture (dense or late-interaction). These choices impose a hard compromise: the most effective pipelines are too slow and expensive to run at scale, while the fastest fail to retrieve evidence from complex documents. Practitioners must therefore choose between missed evidence and unusable latency, with no principled basis for adapting that choice at the query level. We show that this compromise is unnecessary. Not every query requires the same pipeline. Across benchmarks spanning financial and scientific corpora, no static pipeline dominates. We introduce RetrievalRouter, a lightweight query-aware router that learns, from the query text alone, which retrieval pipeline best fits each query. A single tunable parameter exposes the full accuracy-latency frontier, and for every static baseline, RetrievalRouter offers an operating point that is simultaneously more accurate and faster. Against the best static baseline, RetrievalRouter is 2.5% more accurate and 12.4 times faster. Furthermore, compared with prior adaptive strategy selection methods, RetrievalRouter achieves significantly higher nDCG@5 across accuracy-oriented settings, while matching or numerically outperforming them on both nDCG@5 and latency in latency-oriented settings. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/emrekuruu/retrieval-router.

</details>


### [334] [Data Citation for Large Language Models: A Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25663)
*Gianmaria Silvello*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文指出大语言模型的数据引用（data citation）是不同于文档级引用归因（document-level citation grounding）的开放挑战，并提出了三个研究方向：训练数据归因、推理时数据引用，以及知识图谱事实引用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将大语言模型的引用仅视为验证机制，且只针对文本文档。然而学术引用的功能还包括信用（credit）和来源（provenance），并且同样适用于数据。数据引用问题不同于文档引用且更难解决，因此需要专门研究。

Method: 论文提出三大研究方向：(1) 训练数据归因——将影响估计（influence estimates）转化为被模型参数吸收的语料库引用；(2) 推理时数据引用——在正确的粒度和固定性（granularity and fixity）上识别数据集、子集和查询结果；(3) 知识图谱事实引用——定义单个三元组引用的含义以及信用如何沿来源传播。

Result: 该论文为立场/愿景类工作，提出数据引用作为大语言模型领域的一个开放研究挑战，并明确了三个具体的研究方向及所需的跨学科合作（数据库、信息检索、知识表示和人工智能社区）。

Conclusion: 大语言模型的数据引用要使输出可验证、来源可追溯、信用能到达数据创建者和策展者，需要数据库、信息检索、知识表示和人工智能领域共同推进。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly mediate access to information, and a growing body of work asks whether they cite the sources behind their outputs. That work treats citation as a verification device and applies it to textual documents. Scholarly citation serves two further functions, credit and provenance, and it applies to data as much as to text. This paper argues that data citation for large language models is an open challenge, distinct from document-level citation grounding and harder to solve. We ask how such models should cite data so that outputs stay verifiable, provenance stays traceable, and credit reaches data creators and curators. We set out three research directions. Training data attribution has to turn influence estimates into references for corpora absorbed into model parameters. Data citation at inference time has to identify datasets, subsets, and query results at the right granularity and fixity. Citing knowledge graph facts has to define what a reference to a single triple denotes and how credit propagates along provenance. Progress on all three depends on joint work across the database, information retrieval, knowledge representation, and artificial intelligence communities.

</details>


### [335] [Hamiltonian Spectral-Temporal Dissipative Dynamics for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25755)
*Shuiying Liao,P. Y. Mok*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出哈密顿谱推荐器（HSR），将序列推荐中的用户偏好演化重构为耗散哈密顿系统，通过频域闭式解、可学习的耗散机制和局部脉冲精化模块，同时捕捉惯性演化、周期模式和突发行為，在三个基准数据集上超越基于Transformer和状态空间模型的最先进推荐方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列推荐模型大多将用户偏好演化视为一阶过程，即下一状态仅依赖于当前潜在表示，但真实用户行为包含惯性、周期性和突发现象等更丰富的动力学特征，无法被一阶假设完整捕捉。

Method: 将偏好演化建模为潜在相空间（位置表示稳定偏好，动量表示短期倾向）中的耗散哈密顿系统；利用线性时不变结构的频域闭式解；可学习耗散机制捕捉兴趣自然衰减；短期局部脉冲精化模块建模稀疏交互日志中的突变行为。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，HSR持续优于最先进的基于Transformer和基于状态空间模型（SSM）的推荐器。

Conclusion: 通过二阶动力系统视角重建序列推荐，HSR能联合建模全局周期模式、惯性演化和局部冲击这三种现有序列模型代表性不足的现象，从而显著提升推荐性能。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation requires understanding how user preferences evolve over time, yet most existing models treat such evolution as a first order process where the next state depends solely on the current latent representation. Nevertheless, real user behavior often exhibits richer dynamics, including inertia, periodicity, and sudden shifts that cannot be fully captured by these first order assumptions. Motivated by these behavioral characteristics, we reconceptualize sequential recommendation through the lens of second order dynamical systems and introduce the Hamiltonian Spectral Recommender (HSR), which recasts preference evolution as a dissipative Hamiltonian system in a latent phase space of position (stable preference) and momentum (short-term tendency). The linear time-invariant structure of the governing equation admits a closed-form solution in the frequency domain. A learnable dissipation mechanism further captures natural interest decay, while a short local impulse refinement module models abrupt behavioral fluctuations commonly observed in sparse interaction logs. This design jointly accounts for global periodic patterns, inertial evolution, and localized shocks, where three phenomena that are underrepresented in existing sequential models. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that HSR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art Transformer-based and state space model (SSM)-based recommenders.

</details>


### [336] [PUMA: Post-Hoc Sparsification of Universal Multimodal Embeddings for Efficient Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25780)
*Matteo Attimonelli,Alessandro De Bellis,Franco Maria Nardini,Claudio Pomo,Cosimo Rulli,Rossano Venturini,Tommaso Di Noia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PUMA是一种无需重新训练骨干网络的稀疏自编码器方法，将通用多模态嵌入映射为紧凑稀疏编码，在保持密集点积几何结构的同时将向量存储降低8-16倍，并在大型候选池上实现最高25倍的加速检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多模态嵌入器（如Qwen3-VL-Embedding）支持跨文本、图像及组合查询的检索，但其稠密表示带来高昂的内存和推理成本。事后稀疏化虽可降低成本，但在多模态检索领域尚未被充分探索。

Method: PUMA包含两阶段训练：预训练阶段保留稠密点积几何结构，之后对稀疏编码器进行检索任务微调。通过稀疏自编码器将稠密嵌入映射为稀疏编码，无需重新训练骨干网络。

Result: 在五个基准上（涵盖文本到图像和组合图像检索），PUMA在Qwen3-VL-Embedding-2B上于四/五个数据集中与稠密检索统计上无差异或更优。向量存储降低8-16倍（FP32），在更大的候选池上比精确稠密评分快25倍。

Conclusion: PUMA实现了高效的多模态稠密检索稀疏化，并识别出两个失败模式：预TopK支持不足和检索不匹配的活跃支持，为后续改进提供了方向。

Abstract: Universal multimodal embedders enable retrieval across text, image, and combined queries, but their dense representations incur high memory and inference costs. Post-hoc sparsification could reduce these costs but remains underexplored for multimodal retrieval. We introduce PUMA, a sparse autoencoder recipe that maps universal multimodal embeddings to compact sparse codes without retraining the backbone: a pretraining stage preserves dense dot-product geometry, after which the sparse encoder is fine-tuned for retrieval. We evaluate on five benchmarks covering text-to-image and composed image retrieval. On Qwen3-VL-Embedding-2B, PUMA is statistically indistinguishable from or improves over dense retrieval on four of five datasets. We further identify two failure modes of post-hoc sparsification: insufficient pre-TopK support and retrieval-misaligned active support. PUMA reduces vector storage by 8-16x (FP32) and is up to 25x faster than exact dense scoring on larger candidate pools, enabling efficient multimodal retrieval.

</details>


### [337] [PlanSightRAG: A Visual-First Multimodal RAG for Automating Question Answering and Compliance Checking for Civil Standard Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26091)
*Nabaraj Subedi,Shuvo Dip Datta,Ahmed Abdelaty,Shivanand Venkanna Sheshappanavar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出PlanSightRAG，一种视觉优先的多模态RAG框架，直接对图纸图像进行索引和推理，结合ColNomic-3B多向量检索、Planner-Retriever-Auditor-Synthesizer智能体流程及MaxSim热力图证据追踪。在5个州交通部标准图纸（1898页）的4056对基准上达到91.47%的零样本Recall@5，并在密歇根州DOT预留语料上达到91.40%。合成合规性图纸中Qwen2.5-VL-72B仅在预解析规则阈值下达到100%判定准确率，而无需预解析规则的OCR基线为76.4%。最后展示了无需人工规则即可从规范语料库中自动提取数值限值实现视觉规则落地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统土木基础设施合规性检查依赖工程师人工阅读遗留2D图纸，OCR自动化方法丢失了几何和布局信息，这些信息对图纸理解至关重要。因此需要一种能够直接对图像进行索引和推理的多模态方法。

Method: 提出PlanSightRAG框架：(1) 视觉优先，直接对图纸图像建立索引而非OCR文本；(2) 使用ColNomic-3B多向量检索；(3) 采用Planner-Retriever-Auditor-Synthesizer四阶段智能体流程；(4) 利用MaxSim热力图提供证据追溯；(5) 构建4056对5个州DOT标准图纸基准数据集；(6) 使用Qwen2.5-VL-72B进行合规性判定，并演示从规范文本中自动提取数值限值实现视觉规则落地。

Result: 零样本检索Recall@5达91.47%（综合基准）和91.40%（密歇根州DOT预留语料）；合成合规图纸上Qwen2.5-VL-72B在预解析规则阈值下100%判定准确率，非VLM OCR基线为76.4%；无需人工规则即可从规范语料自动提取数值限值。

Conclusion: 视觉优先的多模态RAG框架在图纸检索与合规性检查上显著优于OCR文本方案，且能实现自主视觉规则落地。但研究也揭示了当前局限性：视觉语言模型在无预解析规则时准确率大幅下降，说明规则解析仍是关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Civil infrastructure compliance checking has long relied on engineers manually reading legacy 2D plans; however, OCR-based automation strips away the geometry and layout essential for interpreting these plans. We present a Visual-First Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) framework called PlanSightRAG. It indexes and reasons directly over plan imagery, integrates a ColNomic-3B multi-vector retrieval, an agentic Planner-Retriever-Auditor-Synthesizer, and MaxSim heatmaps as an evidence trail. We introduce a 4,056-pair benchmark from five state Departments of Transportation (DOT) standard plans (1,898 pages). PlanSightRAG achieves 91.47% Recall@5 on zero-shot retrieval, while on a held-out Michigan DOT corpus, it achieves 91.40%. On synthetic, parametrically-generated compliance drawings, our Qwen2.5-VL-72B pipeline reaches 100% verdict accuracy only when supplied a pre-resolved rule threshold, a controlled ceiling that a non-VLM OCR baseline already reaches at 76.4%. Finally, we demonstrate autonomous visual rule-grounding by extracting numeric limits directly from a specification corpus without any human-supplied rules.

</details>


### [338] [VisDocAgentBench: Benchmarking Agents for Visually Rich Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17889)
*Lexiang Hu,Yanzhao Zhang,Mingxin Li,Dingkun Long,Yikang Li,Fuwei Zhang,Yisen Wang,Zhouchen Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了VisDocAgentBench基准，一个用于比较静态检索与智能体检索的封闭语料库基准，包含100个文档、2375页和120个查询，跨越直接、单桥和双桥证据结构。实验表明静态视觉检索在直接项上Recall@1达97.50%，但双桥项仅为2.50%；智能体检索可恢复部分损失，视觉检索的最佳R@1达67.50%，优于OCR文本的37.50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉丰富文档的检索评估通常基于一次性查询-页面匹配，而智能体检索基准多关注下游问答或报告生成，文档排序在迭代证据获取场景下的表现未被充分探索。

Method: 构建封闭语料库基准VisDocAgentBench，包含关系保持的构造方法生成语义、关系和视觉三类查询，进行全文档审查和硬负样本验证；在共享排序输出合约下比较静态与智能体检索，并通过消融实验分析迭代搜索、页面检查、完整支持上下文等能力的影响。

Result: 静态视觉检索在直接项上Recall@1达97.50%但双桥项仅2.50%；智能体检索中视觉检索最佳R@1为67.50%（OCR文本为37.50%），所有规划器在视觉检索下表现更好；消融实验确认迭代搜索和页面检查是关键能力，提供完整支持上下文可改善两条路线的排序。

Conclusion: 查询-目标匹配在依赖语料库上下文的证据结构中存在严重局限，需结合保留模态的发现能力与证据导向的验证能力的检索智能体。

Abstract: Visually rich documents encode relevance through language, layout, structured visual elements, and corpus context, yet retrieval is typically evaluated by one-shot query--page matching. Agentic-search benchmarks usually score downstream question answering or report generation, leaving document ranking under iterative evidence acquisition underexplored. We introduce VisDocAgentBench, a closed-corpus benchmark comparing static and agentic retrieval under a shared ranked-output contract. It contains 2,375 pages from 100 documents and 120 unique-target queries balanced across direct, one-bridge, and two-bridge evidence structures. Relation-preserving construction yields semantic, relational, and visual queries, followed by full-document review and hard-negative validation. A strong late-interaction visual retriever reaches 97.50% Recall@1 on direct items but 2.50% on two-bridge items, exposing the limits of query--target matching when relevance depends on corpus context. Agents recover much of this loss, but planner choice and retrieval representation remain decisive. Every planner performs better with visual retrieval, whose best R@1 reaches 67.50% versus 37.50% for OCR-text. Ablations identify iterative search and page inspection as consequential capabilities, and providing the complete support context improves ranking on both routes. Trace analysis localizes the remaining losses to target discovery, candidate examination, and evidence-role integration. These findings motivate retrieval agents that combine modality-preserving discovery with evidence-directed verification.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [339] [Secret MCP: Evidence-Bounded and Context-Isolated Design Specification Generation from Web Screenshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24944)
*Yeongjin Jo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍Secret MCP，一个开源本地系统，用于从单个公开网页参考生成可审计的设计规格说明。系统通过分离检索、证据准备、模型调用、存储和检查等环节，并采用19章节的规范合同，确保生成的文档不存在跨参考信息污染，实现可追溯、可审计的设计文档生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有截图转代码系统只优化渲染实现，但截图缺少文档结构、交互逻辑、响应式规则和溯源信息，无法区分观察与推测。多参考提示词还存在参考间信息污染的风险，即一个参考的证据或推断可能混入另一个参考的文档中。因此需要一种能够为每个公开网页参考生成独立、可审计设计规格的系统。

Method: Secret MCP采用模块化管道架构：1) 检索与证据准备分离，长截图被调整尺寸并重叠平铺，证据记录保留制备空间和源空间坐标及测量的调色板；2) 采用涵盖页面清单、导航几何、响应式矩阵、组件、可访问性、验收标准等19章节的规范合同；3) 参考按顺序通过采样器接口处理，MCP适配器对每个参考发送一次sampling/createMessage请求且includeContext设为none，新鲜的进程适配器提供更强的隔离边界；4) 证据中的声明明确标注为测量、观察、推断或未知。

Result: 在commit c130c9c上进行了两级评估。实时检索与夹具模型集成测试选择两个参考（排除第三个），准备了9张证据图像，发出2次采样请求，生成两份文档且零跨参考标识符出现。静态审计三个外部生成的设计索引发现：所有文档均包含全部19个必需章节、每个文档一个唯一参考标识符、8个页面规格、1,943个像素值测量和319个颜色字面量。测试验证了编排不变量和语法合同合规性，而非语义或视觉重建精度。

Conclusion: Secret MCP通过采样器抽象保持了跨直接模型API和传输的边界，尽管MCP采样在2026年将被弃用。该系统建立了编排不变量和语法合同合规性，是一项关注设计文档可审计性和参考隔离的基础性工作，其验证聚焦于系统架构约束而非视觉重建质量。

Abstract: Screenshot-to-code systems optimize for rendered implementations, but screenshots omit document structure, interaction logic, responsive rules, and provenance needed to distinguish observation from guesswork. Multi-reference prompts also risk contaminating one reference with evidence or inferences from another. We present Secret MCP, an open-source local system that produces one auditable design specification per public web reference. It separates retrieval, evidence preparation, model invocation, storage, and inspection. Long captures are resized and tiled with overlap; evidence records preserve prepared- and source-space coordinates and a measured color palette. A 19-section contract covers page inventories, navigation geometry, responsive matrices, components, accessibility, acceptance criteria, and explicit labels for measured, observed, inferred, and unknown claims. References are processed sequentially through a sampler interface. The evaluated MCP adapter sends one sampling/createMessage request per reference with includeContext set to none; a fresh-process adapter provides a stronger boundary.
  We evaluate commit c130c9c at two levels. A live retrieval and fixture-model integration run selected two references after excluding a third, prepared nine evidence images, issued two sampling requests, and produced two documents with zero cross-reference identifier occurrences. A static audit of three externally generated design indexes found all 19 required sections in every document, one unique reference identifier per document, eight page specifications, 1,943 pixel-valued measurements, and 319 color literals. These tests establish orchestration invariants and syntactic contract compliance, not semantic or visual reconstruction accuracy. The sampler abstraction preserves these boundaries across direct model APIs and transports despite MCP sampling's 2026 deprecation.

</details>


### [340] [The Evolution of Binary Decompilation in the Modern Era: A Taxonomy, Literature Review, and Future Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24955)
*Omar Abusabha,Sungjae Hwang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对过去数十年间的反编译研究进行了系统性综述，构建了当代研究方法论的全面分类体系，并审视了评估指标、工具和基准的趋势，揭示了缺乏可靠真值和标准化基准等关键挑战，最后指出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反编译已成为软件工程和安全分析中的基础技术，并正通过机器学习方法的整合而不断推进。然而该领域缺乏系统性的综述来梳理方法论、评估实践及存在的挑战，因此需要进行全面回顾以指引后续研究。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法，对过去数十年间发表的反编译研究进行收集与整理，在此基础上开发出一套涵盖当代研究方法的综合分类学（taxonomy），并进一步分析评估指标、工具和基准的使用趋势。

Result: 综述揭示了该领域的关键挑战，包括缺乏可靠的地面真值（ground truth）以及缺少标准化基准，这些问题阻碍了不同方法之间的严格比较。

Conclusion: 文章指出需解决现有评估基础薄弱的问题，并提出了未来研究方向以推动反编译研究的发展。

Abstract: Decompilation has become a foundational technique in software engineering and security analysis, and it is now advancing through the integration of modern machine learning (ML) approaches. This article presents a systematic review of decompilation studies published over the past decades and develops a comprehensive taxonomy of methodologies employed in contemporary research. We further examine trends in evaluation metrics, tools, and benchmarks used to assess state-of-the-art approaches. Our review reveals key challenges, such as the lack of reliable ground truth and the absence of standardized benchmarks, which hinder rigorous comparison. Finally, we outline future research directions.

</details>


### [341] [Evaluating and Preventing Security Smells in AI-Generated Ansible Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24962)
*Pandu Ranga Reddy Konala,Vimal Kumar,David Bainbridge,Junaid Haseeb*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次评估16个AI模型生成Ansible基础设施即代码（IaC）的安全性，发现所有模型在无安全引导时均产生含安全坏味的代码，无法通过CIS合规验证。研究者提出基于扩展CO-STAR框架的提示工程方法，将Ansible最佳实践和CIS基准融入提示，使4个模型生成合规代码，最佳模型达到95%-100%的CIS合规率，比人类开发者（23%-43%）提升4倍，代码质量提升19%-49%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代码助手越来越多地用于生成基础设施即代码（IaC），但尚无研究考察这些代码是否满足安全要求。由于基础设施代码中的安全坏味会传播到部署系统，造成不安全、不可信的基础设施，因此亟需评估和解决这一问题。

Method: 评估16个AI模型生成Apache Tomcat v10和MongoDB v7的Ansible角色，分析278个Ansible角色对照CIS基准；提出一种集成Ansible最佳实践和CIS基准的提示工程方法，基于扩展的CO-STAR框架实现安全坏味的"合成前预防"而非"部署后检测"。

Result: 无安全引导时16个模型全部生成含安全坏味的代码；应用新方法后4/16模型生成合规代码，最佳模型达95%-100% CIS合规率（人类为23%-43%），代码质量提升19%-49%。其余12个模型失败原因是无法遵循多约束指令而非无法生成代码。

Conclusion: 对于能力足够的模型，该安全方法无需重新训练，可通过系统提示直接采纳，是提升AI生成基础设施代码安全性的有效途径。这表明提示工程可在不增加模型训练成本的情况下显著改善IaC安全性。

Abstract: AI coding assistants generate Infrastructure as Code, yet no work has examined whether this code meets security requirements. This matters because security smells in infrastructure code propagate to deployed systems, producing infrastructure that is insecure and untrustworthy. We evaluate 16 AI models generating Ansible roles for Apache Tomcat v10 and MongoDB v7, analysing 278 Ansible roles against CIS benchmarks. Without security guidance, all 16 AI models produced code containing security smells, resulting in vulnerable infrastructure that fails compliance verification and underperforms code written by human developers. We introduce an approach integrating Ansible best practices and CIS benchmarks into prompts through an extended CO-STAR framework, enabling security smell prevention during synthesis rather than detection after deployment. When this approach is applied, 4 out of 16 models generate compliant code, with the leading model achieving 95%-100% CIS compliance, a fourfold improvement over humans at 23%-43%, with overall code quality improving by 19%-49%. The remaining 12 models fail not because they cannot generate code but because they cannot follow instructions with multiple constraints. For capable models, the approach requires no retraining and can be adopted through system prompts.

</details>


### [342] [ARISMA: Guidelines for AI- and LLM-Assisted Systematic Reviews, Scoping Reviews, and Mapping Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25050)
*Mahyar Tourchi Moghaddam,Mina Alipour*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ARISMA标准，这是一个AI报告与集成标准，用于系统综述和证据综合中负责任地使用AI。该框架将AI视为可检查、可基准测试、可记录和可逆的辅助工具，而非自主审阅者，核心原则是所有关键科学决策必须保持人类可解释、可审计和可问责。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着检索量、更新周期和综合要求的不断扩展，全手动工作流越来越难以完成证据综合。虽然AI/ML/LLM正迅速进入综述实践，但实证证据不均衡且不足以证明无约束自动化的合理性。现有标准（如PRISMA 2020、PRISMA-S等）虽然重要，但缺乏一个端到端的操作标准来规定AI使用何时在方法上适当、如何验证、哪些决策必须由人主导，以及如何报告AI参与以便读者审计。

Method: ARISMA围绕一个核心原则构建：每个关键科学决策必须保持人类可解释、可审计和可问责。论文贡献了：生命周期分类法、过程指南、综述流程的逐步推荐、治理与溯源模型、工具支持框架、AI集成报告检查表和验证矩阵。同时涵盖法律、隐私、基础设施和可持续性考量。框架通过结构化专家咨询进行迭代完善。

Result: 最终产物是一个实用且可审计的负责任AI辅助证据综合指南，强调AI在综述过程中的透明性、可验证性和可逆性。

Conclusion: ARISMA填补了现有报告标准在AI集成方面的空白，为在证据综合中负责任地使用AI提供了端到端的操作标准和审计框架。

Abstract: Systematic reviews, scoping reviews, mapping studies, and related evidence syntheses are increasingly difficult to conduct with fully manual workflows as search volumes, update cycles, and synthesis requirements continue to expand. At the same time, artificial intelligence, machine learning, and large language models are rapidly entering review practice across query formulation, screening, extraction, categorization, appraisal support, and reporting. Yet the empirical evidence remains uneven, task-dependent, and insufficient to justify unconstrained automation. Existing standards such as PRISMA 2020, PRISMA-S, PRISMA-ScR, PRISMA-P, PRESS, and SWiM remain essential, but none provides an end-to-end operational standard for when AI use is methodologically appropriate, how it should be validated, which review decisions must remain human-led, and how AI involvement should be reported so that readers can audit it. This paper proposes ARISMA, an AI Reporting and Integration standard for Systematic Methods and Analysis. ARISMA treats AI as an inspected, benchmarked, logged, and reversible assistant rather than an autonomous reviewer. It is built around one governing principle: every consequential scientific decision must remain human-interpretable, human-auditable, and human-accountable. The paper contributes a lifecycle taxonomy, process guidance, stepwise recommendations across the review pipeline, a governance and provenance model, a tool-support framework, an AI-integrated reporting checklist, and a validation matrix. It also addresses legal, privacy, infrastructure, and sustainability considerations. The framework was iteratively refined through structured expert consultation. The result is a practical and auditable guideline for responsible AI-assisted evidence synthesis.

</details>


### [343] [Model-Based Agentic Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25174)
*James C. Davis,Kelechi Kalu,Huiyun Peng,Parth V. Patil*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出MAGE（Model-Based Agentic Software Engineering），一个框架和理论，用于从通用智能构建可信赖的自主性。它通过将最小的有目的表示外化，并赋予已解决的义务以相称的权威（通过约束、传感器、验证器和门控），来解决表示问题和权威问题，将不确定的意图保持开放，把重复的构建和判断转化为可继承的工程结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体增加了实现能力，但不会自动使项目意图、系统结构或验收证据明确化。当实现相对于工程判断变得丰富时，稀缺工作转向选择有用的抽象、产生证据以及确定哪些义务支配验收。现有工作流（更大提示、仓库检索、逐变更审查）仍要求智能体和工程师重建关键属性。

Method: MAGE将外部化知识、受限行动、独立评估和保留的人类权威组合成受治理的工程环境。它通过外部化回答工程问题所需的最小有目的表示，并通过约束、传感器、验证器和门控给予已判定义务相称权威。该方法源自纵向案例，并通过6个独立报告的工业案例进行完善。

Result: 跨这些来源，MAGE解释了外部化知识、受限行动、独立评估和保留的人类权威如何组合成一个受治理的工程环境，并提出了检验该环境何时将通用智能转化为持久工程进展的测试方法。

Conclusion: MAGE提出了一种替代路径：与其扩大提示或增加检索，不如将重复的推理和判断固化为持久的工程结构，供后续工作继承，从而在保持人类权威的同时实现可信赖的智能体自主性。

Abstract: Coding agents increase implementation capacity without automatically making project intent, system structure, or acceptance evidence explicit. As implementation becomes abundant relative to engineering judgment, the scarce work shifts toward choosing useful abstractions, producing evidence, and determining which obligations govern acceptance. Existing workflows address parts of this gap through larger prompts, repository retrieval, or perchange review, but still require agents and engineers to reconstruct consequential properties.
  As an alternative, we present Model-Based Agentic Software Engineering (MAGE). MAGE is a framework and a theory for building trustworthy autonomy from commodity intelligence. MAGE addresses a representation problem and an authority problem: it externalizes the smallest purposeful representation needed to answer an engineering question, then gives settled obligations proportionate authority through constraints, sensors, validators, and gates. It keeps uncertain intent open and turns recurring reconstruction and judgment into durable engineering structure that later work can inherit. We developed MAGE from a longitudinal case and refined it through six independently reported industrial accounts. Across these sources, MAGE explains how externalized knowledge, bounded action, independent evaluation, and retained human authority can compose into a governed engineering environment, and proposes tests of when that environment turns commodity intelligence into durable engineering progress.

</details>


### [344] [SPECMINE: A Large-Scale Corpus of Spec-Driven Development Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25202)
*Shyam Agarwal,Bogdan Vasilescu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SPECMINE是一个大规模的语料库，通过两次普查（广义普查470,795个spec文件覆盖73,030个仓库和17种工具；Kiro普查98,574个文件覆盖12,910个仓库）系统性地捕捉了GitHub公共仓库中的Spec-Driven Development (SDD)实践，并配套5,992个相关PR及241万个类型化引用索引，使研究者首次能够在大型规模上研究AI代理时代的软件规格定义方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自2025年以来，Spec-Driven Development (SDD)作为一种新兴实践迅速兴起，大量工具（如GitHub Spec Kit、OpenSpec、AWS Kiro等）相继出现，但这些工具产生的规格工件从未在大规模上被研究过。此外，"规格如何转化为代码"这一问题也尚未得到解答。

Method: SPECMINE通过两个普查构建语料库：1）广义普查，收集spec.md/specs.md文件（470,795个文件、73,030个仓库，归属17种工具）；2）Kiro普查，针对Kiro独特的requirements/design/tasks三层结构（98,574个文件、12,910个仓库）。每个规格文件都配有完整的仓库元数据、全部提交历史和解析后的文档结构。此外，对11种工具中星级≥10的仓库进行了PR全面扫描，捕捉了5,992个涉及规格修改的PR及其变更集，并构建了包含2,421,323个类型化引用的索引，提供从规格到代码的独立关联。

Result: 构建了一个前所未有的SDD语料库，包含两个层次的普查数据、详细的PR变更记录以及大规模的类型化引用索引，使"规格与实现在同一PR中共同变更"这一最简单工作流可以直接被观测，为研究AI代理时代的软件开发提供了数据基础。

Conclusion: SPECMINE首次让社区能够在大规模数据上研究SDD实践中软件规格是如何被定义和编写的，填补了该领域的空白，为理解AI代理时代的软件开发流程提供了关键资源。

Abstract: Spec-Driven Development (SDD) is a fast-emerging practice in which a structured natural-language specification, written by a developer, or (more often) drafted by an AI tool and then curated by the developer, drives an AI coding agent's implementation. A wave of tooling (GitHub Spec Kit [3], OpenSpec [4], AWS Kiro [5], and dozens of others) has appeared since 2025, yet the artifacts these tools produce have never been studied at scale. We present SPECMINE, a corpus that captures SDD in public GitHub repositories through two censuses: a broad census of spec.md/specs.md files covering most tools (470,795 files across 73,030 repositories, attributed to 17 named tools), and a Kiro census of its distinct requirements/design/tasks layout (98,574 files across 12,910 repositories). Each spec is enriched with full repository metadata, complete commit history, and parsed document structure. How a spec becomes code is itself an open question, so for 11 tools we sweep every pull request that touches a spec in their repositories with at least ten stars, capturing 5,992 such PRs across 581 repositories with their changesets. That makes the simplest workflow, spec and implementation changing together in one PR, directly observable, and a census-wide index of 2,421,323 typed references (1.28M to code files, 863k to sibling documents, 152k to PRs, 62k refs, 43k branches, 22k issues) gives a second, independent link from spec to code. SPECMINE lets the community study, for the first time, how software is specified in the age of AI agents.

</details>


### [345] [A Few Pages of Markdown: Committed AI Configuration and Lower Quality Cost after Coding-Agent Adoption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25241)
*Yegor Denisov-Blanch,Shyam Agarwal,Pavel Azaletskiy,Hao He,Rylan Schaeffer,Brando Miranda,Bogdan Vasilescu,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RAMP（仓库AI成熟度画像）模型，一个四级累积成熟度模型，基于团队提交的版本控制工件来配置AI工具。通过441个仓库的分析，RAMP层级形成累积量表，独立人工标注在97%样本上复现。采用是累积、只进不退、设置即忘的（73.8%工件只提交一次从未修改）。在每层内重新估计代理采纳面板，代理加速开发（28-38%更多提交），但质量分化：在代理优先仓库中，没有AI配置的仓库认知复杂度增加约两倍（+53% vs +27%），静态分析警告增加1.7倍。由于成熟度是观察性的，相关性可能来自工程纪律或模型能力，作者将发现视为假设生成性的，并发布RAMP作为可复用工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅报告编码代理采纳的平均效应，掩盖了团队之间的巨大差异。作者希望揭示代理采纳对不同成熟度团队的影响差异，特别是技术债务方面的分化。

Method: 提出RAMP四级累积成熟度模型，基于版本控制工件（行为规则、编码标准、命名代理定义、多代理编排）分类仓库；在441个仓库上验证模型，并通过独立人工标注复现验证；在每层内重新估计既有代理采纳面板数据。

Result: RAMP层级有效且可复现（97%标注一致）；代理在任何成熟度下都加速开发（28-38%更多提交）；但质量上，在代理优先仓库中，缺乏AI配置的仓库认知复杂度增加约2倍（+53% vs +27%），静态分析警告增加1.7倍。

Conclusion: 编码代理的加速效应普遍存在，但技术债务效应因团队成熟度而异；未配置AI工具的团队面临更严重的代码质量退化。发现是假设生成性的，RAMP作为可复用研究工具发布。

Abstract: Coding agents increase development velocity but also technical debt. Prior work reports only average effects across adopters, hiding wide differences between teams. We introduce RAMP (Repository AI Maturity Profile), a four-level cumulative maturity model grounded in version-controlled artifacts that teams commit to configure AI tools. RAMP runs from behavioral rules and coding standards through named agent definitions to multi-agent orchestration, with observed practice concentrated in the first three levels. Across 441 repositories the levels behave as a cumulative scale, and independent human annotation reproduces RAMP's repository-level labels on 97% of a held-out sample. Adoption is cumulative, forward-only, and set-and-forget: 73.8% of artifacts are committed once and never modified. Re-estimating an existing agent-adoption panel within each stratum, agents accelerate development regardless of maturity (28-38% more commits), but quality diverges: among agent-first repositories, where the contrast is identified, those without committed AI configuration show roughly twice the increase in cognitive complexity (+53% versus +27%) and 1.7x the increase in static-analysis warnings. Because maturity is observational, correlated engineering discipline or model capability may explain part of the gap; we present these findings as hypothesis-generating and release RAMP as a reusable instrument.

</details>


### [346] [Metis: Typed Runtime Mediation for Tool-Using Software Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25322)
*Jun Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍Metis，一个多提供商运行时系统，将概率模型输出转化为类型化事件，在执行外部效果前进行权限决策、干扰分类、终端结果和生命周期转换的显式处理。在30个真实I/O对上，四类中介将中位耗时从25.958ms降至14.146ms（平均差异-12.295ms），所有配对均更快。十例故障矩阵揭示了重复标识和回滚限制，子边界消融实验验证了权限门控和注册表保护的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件代理将概率模型输出连接到改变存储库、流程、网络和图形应用的操作中，需要一个运行时系统来管理和中介这些模型输出到外部效果的执行路径，确保安全性、可检查性和可追溯性。

Method: Metis采用多提供商运行时架构，在调用到达外部效果之前，将提供商流转换为类型化事件。执行路径显式化权限决策、干扰类别、终端结果和生命周期转换。通过门控加注册表的双层保护实现子边界控制，并通过四类中介（mediation）机制优化并发执行。

Result: 跨30个真实I/O对，四类中介将中位耗时从25.958ms（强制串行化）降至14.146ms，平均配对差异-12.295ms（95%自举区间[-12.968, -11.694]），全部配对均更快。十例故障矩阵揭示重复标识符与回滚限制。子边界消融实验表明完整门控加注册表条件阻止未授权效果并隐藏全部五个逃逸工具；仅决策的权限预知在五种调用路径上匹配全部十例声明案例。五种模型条件在固定的Read标记协议上3/3试验均完成。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持关于调度、权限路由、子权限和提供商验证迹闭合的有界声明。但不建立模型能力、语义安全、回滚或其他运行时优势的声明。

Abstract: Software agents connect probabilistic model output to operations that change repositories, processes, networks, and graphical applications. We present Metis, a multi-provider runtime that converts provider streams into typed events before admitted calls reach external effects. Its execution path makes permission decisions, interference classes, terminal results, and lifecycle transitions explicit and inspectable. We evaluate these mechanisms on frozen source artifacts. Across 30 matched real-I/O pairs, four-class mediation reduced median elapsed time from 25.958 ms under forced serialization to 14.146 ms. The mean paired difference was -12.295 ms (95% bootstrap interval [-12.968, -11.694]), with mediation faster in all pairs. A ten-case fault matrix exposed duplicate-identifier and rollback limits. In a child-boundary ablation, the full gate-plus-registry condition blocked the declared unauthorized effect and hid all five escape tools. Removing both protections reversed both observations. A decision-only permission oracle matched all ten declared cases across five invocation routes. Five model conditions also completed a fixed Read-marker protocol in 3/3 trials each. These results support bounded claims about dispatch, permission routing, child authority, and provider-valid trace closure. They do not establish model competence, semantic safety, rollback, or superiority over another runtime.

</details>


### [347] [Point-in-Time Audit Before Alpha: Public-Archive Availability and a Negative Matched-Budget Study on BTC Perpetual Futures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25348)
*Baocheng Zeng,Jinhao Yang,Peilin Han,Kangnan He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对公开的币安BTCUSDT永续合约数据进行了审计，发现数据存在严重缺失（最长断档304.6天），修订后保留727天完整数据。审计器能100%检测出违规模板并正确拒绝合法模板，显著降低了零信号路径的假阳性率（从0.2910降至0.0625）。但在历史留出集上所有策略的净夏普比率为负，因此报告的是有范围界定的负面结果，而非盈利性或代理优越性主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因子研究往往只关注文件是否存在，而忽略了在决策时点数据是否可用且可执行这一关键前提。作者希望通过严格的事件/发布/可用时间审计，验证公开加密货币数据是否能支撑可复现的因子研究。

Method: 采用事件时间、发布时间和可用时间三重维度审计币安BTCUSDT永续合约数据。区分提议式审计与确定性审计/评估/留出访问。构建审计器在40个违反模板和40个合法模板上验证，并在10条零信号路径上检验全部审计能力，最后在历史留出集上进行一次性的评估。

Result: 最初的五分钟无缺口要求（交易、标记、指数、持仓量）失败，最长断档304.6天。修订后的掩码保留727个完整UTC日（436/145/146天划分）。审计器检出40/40违规并拒绝0/40合法模板。全部审计将平均假通过率从0.2910降至0.0625。审计算法在匹配预算下与随机搜索持平，所有策略在留出集上IC为正但净夏普为负。

Conclusion: 公开币安数据在严格时间审计下存在严重可用性问题，即使经过修订也无法支撑盈利性主张。最终结论是一个有范围界定的负面结果：在主要成本下，所有评估路径的净夏普均为负，不应声称盈利性或代理优越性。

Abstract: Public cryptocurrency archives may appear usable when files exist, although factor research requires observations available and executable at each decision time. We audit public Binance BTCUSDT USD-M perpetual-futures data using event, publication, and availability times and separate proposal from deterministic auditing, evaluation, and holdout access. An initial gapless five-minute requirement for trade, mark, index, and open interest failed: the longest unrepaired intersection was 304.5729166666667 days. A disclosed revision made trade, mark, index, and realized funding the core streams and made open interest optional because its publication time was unverified. The revised mask retained 727 complete UTC days and supported a 436/145/146-day train, validation, and historical-holdout split. On 80 frozen known-rule templates, the auditor detected 40/40 violations and rejected 0/40 legal templates. Across ten null-signal paths, full auditing reduced mean false passes from 0.2910 to 0.0625. Under matched valid-candidate budgets, the audited adaptive agent tied random search and did not establish superiority. In the one-time historical holdout, all evaluated runs had positive IC but negative net Sharpe under primary costs. We therefore report a scoped negative result rather than a profitability or agent-superiority claim.

</details>


### [348] [Retry Amplification in Distributed Systems: A Systematic Analysis of Retry Policies and Their Role in Cascading Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25403)
*Rishabh Mehan,Jasmit Kaur Saluja*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出重试放大因子（RAF）指标来衡量重试策略在部分故障期间产生的额外请求量。通过对200个开源Python微服务项目的分析，发现显式重试逻辑检出率为11.5%（修正后真实流行率约41%），其中60.9%的配置缺少退避机制。模拟实验表明，在相关故障下，标准重试策略将成功率从55.4%降至41.5%。作者提出自适应重试预算（ARB）机制，在保持接近无重试基线成功率的同时恢复瞬时故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重试机制是弹性分布式系统的标准组件，但各层同时重试的集体行为远不如单客户端指导那样被充分理解。现有重试策略多关注单点配置，缺乏对系统层面重试放大效应的认识。

Method: (1) 提出RAF指标量化重试产生的额外请求量；(2) 对200个开源Python微服务项目进行静态分析检测重试逻辑；(3) 通过n=100次模拟实验评估不同重试策略在相关故障下的表现；(4) 归纳五个重试反模式；(5) 提出自适应重试预算（ARB）机制。

Result: 显式重试检出率11.5%，修正后真实流行率约41%；60.9%的重试配置无退避，仅1/113个生产配置使用随机延迟。相关故障下，朴素标准重试将成功率从55.4%降至41.5%。ARB机制能维持接近无重试基线的成功率并恢复瞬时故障。

Conclusion: 重试行为应作为系统级属性来设计，而非在每个调用点局部配置。预算约束的重试可以在恢复瞬时故障的同时避免相关故障下的雪崩效应。

Abstract: Retry mechanisms are a standard component of resilient distributed systems, but their collective behavior, when every tier in a call path retries concurrently, is less well understood than the per-client guidance that produced them. This paper introduces the retry amplification factor (RAF), a metric quantifying the additional request volume that retry policies generate during partial failures. In a study of 200 open-source Python microservice projects, explicit retry logic is detected in 11.5%, and an audit of our own false negatives places true prevalence near 41%. Among the projects detected, 60.9% contain at least one configuration without backoff, and after manual verification exactly one of 113 production configurations randomizes its delay. We then evaluate these policies in simulation (n = 100 trials per strategy). Under correlated failures, a naive standard retry policy reduces the success rate from 55.4% to 41.5% relative to performing no retries at all. We catalog five recurring anti-patterns, propose Adaptive Retry Budgeting (ARB), and show that budget-constrained retries maintain success rates close to the no-retry baseline while still recovering from transient faults. These results indicate that retry behavior should be designed as a system-level property rather than configured locally at each call site.

</details>


### [349] [RotDroid: Cross-Orientation State Equivalence Testing for Detecting GUI Rotation Bugs in Android Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25425)
*Mengdi Qin,Bo Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RotDroid是一个用于检测Android应用屏幕旋转导致的GUI缺陷的测试框架。它通过生成和变异"状态保持动作序列"(SPS)来构建跨方向语义等价的GUI状态，并利用微调后的视觉语言模型RotVL进行等价性检查，最终在开源和闭源应用中发现94个新bug，其中47个已被开发者确认或修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 屏幕旋转是Android应用的基础交互，但经常引发非崩溃性功能缺陷（NCF），如布局不一致和状态丢失。由于缺乏有效的测试预言（oracle）来检查横竖屏状态之间的等价性，这类缺陷难以自动检测。

Method: 1) 构建RotBench数据集，包含成对的横竖屏GUI状态；2) 开发RotVL，一个针对等价性检查微调的视觉语言模型；3) 设计RotDroid框架，通过生成和变异SPS序列来构造跨方向语义等价的GUI状态，并结合RotVL进行Oracle检查。

Result: RotVL在合成和真实数据集上优于现有最先进模型；在相同预算下，RotDroid比现有技术检测到更多旋转引发的缺陷；在大型开源和闭源应用研究中，报告了94个此前未知的bug，其中47个已被开发者确认或修复。

Conclusion: RotDroid通过创新的跨方向状态等价性检查方法，有效解决了屏幕旋转缺陷自动检测中缺乏可靠测试预言的核心挑战，并在实际应用中展现了显著的实用性和有效性。

Abstract: Screen rotation is a fundamental interaction in Android applications, but it often introduces non-crashing functional failures (NCFs), such as layout inconsistencies and state loss, which are difficult to detect automatically. A key challenge is the lack of effective test oracles for checking cross-orientation state equivalence between portrait and landscape views. We propose RotDroid, a testing framework for detecting GUI rotation bugs via cross-orientation state equivalence. RotDroid generates and mutates State-Preserving action Sequences (SPS) to construct semantically equivalent GUI states across orientations. To support reliable oracle checking, we build RotBench, a dataset of paired portrait-landscape GUI states, and develop RotVL, a vision-language model fine-tuned for equivalence checking. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets show that RotVL outperforms state-of-the-art models, and RotDroid detects more rotation-induced failures than existing techniques under equal budgets. In large-scale studies on open- and closed-source apps, RotDroid reports 94 previously unknown bugs, with 47 confirmed or fixed by developers, demonstrating its practical effectiveness.

</details>


### [350] [A Hybrid Usability Approach for Rating Evaluation of M-Commerce Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25550)
*Ahmad Ibtisam,Bilal Khan,Arshad Ali*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究旨在识别影响M-commerce（移动商务）应用评分的可用性因素，提出一个包含8个因素（可学习性、一致性、人为因素、沟通性、有效性、可操作性、效率、满意度）的混合可用性模型，通过40名用户对5个知名移动应用的数据收集，采用前向逐步多元线性回归构建评分预测模型，并用PRED(x)和K折交叉验证进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动应用的评分是其有用性的重要衡量标准，识别显著影响M-commerce应用评分的可用性因素，有助于改进应用设计和提升用户评分。

Method: 探索现有可用性模型及相关因素和标准，针对5个移动应用（daraz、shophive、home shopping、Symbios、yayvo）进行实证分析；构建包含8个因素的混合可用性模型，每个因素下设若干标准；从每个应用的40名用户收集数据；采用前向逐步多元线性回归（Forward Stepwise Multiple Linear Regression）分析各标准的评分预测模型；使用PRED(x)和K折交叉验证技术对模型进行验证。

Result: 提出了一种基于混合可用性模型的评分预测方法，通过前向逐步回归确定了影响评分的显著可用性因素，并通过PRED(x)和K折验证证明了模型的预测有效性。

Conclusion: 该混合可用性模型能够有效地预测M-commerce应用的评分，为移动商务应用的设计者和开发者提供了重点改进可用性因素的参考依据，从而提升应用的用户体验和评分。

Abstract: The success of any mobile application relies on its usefulness and rating is considered as an important measure in this regard. This research work focuses on identifying usability factors, which contribute significantly towards the rating of M-commerce apps. This work intends to explore existing usability models consisting of different factors along with a set of criteria and evaluate in terms of rating estimation by considering 5 well-known mobile applications, namely (i) daraz, (ii) shophive, (iii) home shopping, (iv) Symbios. (v) yayvo. Then, this work provides a hybrid usability model for rating prediction of M-commerce applications. The initial hybrid usability model comprises of (i) learnability, (ii) consistency, (iii) human factors,(iv)communicativeness,(v)effectiveness, (vi) Operability, (vii) efficiency, (viii) satisfaction. Each factor consists of some criteria. Keeping in view the factors of hybrid usability model, the data was collected from 40 users for each application. Furthermore, Forward Stepwise Multiple Linear Regression based rating prediction model is suggested by analyzing each criterion of all factors of hybrid usability model. Finally, the model is assessed and validated by using PRED(x) and K-fold techniques.

</details>


### [351] [From General Agents to RCA Experts: A Self-Evolving Harness for Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25661)
*Haiyu Huang,Jiewei Lyu,Zhihan Jiang,Jinyang Liu,Xiao He,Tieying Zhang,Wu Xiang,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: OpsHarness是一个自进化的RCA（根因分析）框架，它不重新构建专用RCA智能体，而是复用现代通用LLM智能体的强大能力，通过外部harness层来积累系统特定经验。该框架通过数据平面（分层操作知识+想法卡片工具库）和控制平面（设置、诊断、进化、验证）实现自我进化，在两个公开基准和工业部署中达到59.0%的top-1准确率，相比裸通用智能体提升63.4%，相比基线RCA智能体提升4.02倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文的动机源于对现有LLM进行RCA的两种主流范式的定量研究发现：直接使用通用智能体（如Codex、Claude Code）的方案已逐渐超越从零构建的专用RCA智能体，但其准确率仍不达生产要求。关键差距不在于智能体的通用能力，而在于外部的适应层（harness），因此作者主张RCA研究应聚焦于外部harness的构建，而非重新开发智能体。

Method: OpsHarness采用双平面架构：数据平面将分层操作知识与想法卡片（idea-card）工具库结合，存储和复用诊断经验；控制平面协调设置、诊断、进化、验证四个环节。进化过程中，OpsHarness对比成功与失败的诊断轨迹，将证据转化为原子化提案，并仅通过双门验证机制接纳更新，以防止过拟合和回归。

Result: 在两个公开基准和一个工业部署上，OpsHarness达到59.0%的top-1准确率，相比裸通用智能体提升63.4%，相比基线RCA智能体提升4.02倍。结果表明自进化的外部harness能有效弥补通用智能体在RCA中的不足。

Conclusion: 作者认为LLM驱动的RCA应将重点放在外部harness上，而非重新构建专用智能体。OpsHarness证明了通过自进化机制将诊断经验转化为可复用专业知识，是提升RCA准确率并满足生产需求的有效途径，且使用越多效果越好。

Abstract: Automated root cause analysis (RCA) with large language models (LLMs) has drawn growing attention. Today, SREs typically automate RCA with LLMs in one of two ways: directly using a general-purpose agent (e.g., Codex or Claude Code) for diagnosis, or building a specialized RCA agent from scratch. As mainstream general agents grow more capable and iterate quickly, our quantitative study finds that the former now often surpasses the latter. Its accuracy, however, still falls short of production needs, and this gap stems mainly from the external adaptation layer outside the agent's general capabilities, namely the harness. We therefore argue that LLM-based RCA should focus on this external harness, reusing the strong general capabilities of a modern agent rather than rebuilding an agent from scratch. A key capability of such a harness is to self-evolve, accumulating system-specific experience from past diagnoses so that it gets better the more it is used. We introduce OpsHarness, a self-evolving RCA harness that turns diagnosis experience into reusable expertise. Its data plane combines layered operational knowledge with an idea-card tool library, while its control plane coordinates setup, diagnosis, evolution, and verification. During evolution, OpsHarness contrasts successful and failed trajectories, converts their evidence into atomic proposals, and admits updates only through a dual-gate verification process designed to prevent overfitting and regression. Across two public benchmarks and an industrial deployment, OpsHarness achieves 59.0\% top-1 accuracy, improving over a bare general agent by 63.4\% and over baseline RCA agents by 4.02$\times$.

</details>


### [352] [Predicting Struggling Students in CS1 Programming Using Keystroke-Level Editing Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25769)
*Yasuyo Kofune,Kazumasa Shimari,Kenichi Matsumoto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究在CS1编程练习中，利用击键级日志（keystroke-level logs）早期检测学习困难学生。通过CodeBench平台的实时代码编辑、执行和提交记录，将学生分为突破组（BT）和全卡组（FS），从CodeBench 2019-1学期507名学生的数据中，对比仅执行特征、仅CodeMirror特征及两者组合三种配置，结果显示在最早阶段组合特征预测效果最佳（AUROC 0.674），表明练习最初阶段的击键行为信号能有效预测学生最终能否解决问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分学生在CS1编程练习结束前无法达到正确解决方案，当从成绩或最终结果中发现问题时，教师提供及时干预以帮助学生恢复的机会可能已经错过。因此需要探索能否在练习过程中尽早识别这些挣扎中的学生，以便及时提供教学支持。

Method: 利用CodeBench平台记录的击键级代码编辑事件、执行和提交日志。定义两类学生：Breakthrough（BT）学生（先前提交全为0%但最终提交满分）和Fully Stuck（FS）学生（所有提交均为0%且未获正确解）。对比三种特征配置：执行特征（ExecOnly）、CodeMirror编辑特征（CMOnly）及两者组合（Combined），在每次练习的连续提交阶段评估预测性能，使用AUROC作为评估指标。

Result: 在最早阶段，CMOnly优于ExecOnly（AUROC 0.654 vs 0.575），Combined比ExecOnly提升+0.098（AUROC 0.674）。所有配置中，最早的提交阶段产生最强的预测信号。

Conclusion: 练习一开始的行为信号就包含关于学生最终能否解题的有用线索，击键级编辑日志提供了超越单纯执行日志的额外价值，可用于早期对学生进行优先级排序和干预。

Abstract: This paper investigates the feasibility of early detection of struggling students during CS1 programming exercises using keystroke-level logs. Some students fail to reach a correct solution before the exercise ends, and by the time this becomes apparent from grades or final outcomes, the opportunity for timely instructor support aimed at helping them recover may have passed. We use data from the CodeBench platform, which records real-time code editing events at the keystroke level, alongside execution and submission logs. We define two outcome groups: Breakthrough (BT) students, whose prior submissions all receive 0% and whose final submission achieves full credit, and Fully Stuck (FS) students, whose submissions all receive 0% without reaching a correct solution. To examine this feasibility, we focus on two questions: (RQ1) whether adding keystroke-level editing features improves the prediction of FS students over execution-log features alone, and (RQ2) at which stage BT and FS students can be predicted most accurately. Experiments on the 2019-1 semester of the CodeBench dataset, comprising 507 students, compare three feature configurations: execution-based features (ExecOnly), CodeMirror-based features (CMOnly), and their combination (Combined). We evaluate prediction across successive submission-based stages during each exercise. In the earliest stage, CMOnly outperforms ExecOnly (AUROC 0.654 vs. 0.575), and Combined further improves over ExecOnly by +0.098 (AUROC 0.674). Across all configurations, the earliest stage yielded the strongest predictive signal. These findings indicate that behavioral signals present at the very start of an exercise contain useful clues about whether a student will ultimately solve the problem, and that keystroke-level editing logs provide additional value for early prioritization beyond execution logs alone.

</details>


### [353] [Beyond the Editing Canvas: Evidence Divergence in OOXML-to-LLM Ingestion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25880)
*Side Liu,Jiangpeng Liu,Jinwen Xin,Guojun Peng,Jiang Ming*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文揭示了OOXML（Word/Excel/PowerPoint）文件在LLM管道中的语义完整性假设可能失效的问题。同一规范有效的OOXML文件在Microsoft Office中与在LLM提取管道中呈现不同的"证据视图"，作者称之为"evidence forks"（证据分叉）。系统遍历规范确认了21种证据分叉机制，测试13种提取工具、4个原生LLM API和7个网络聊天机器人，发现API在48-76%的试验中返回了仅提取管道可见而Office界面中不显示的任务相关"陷阱"信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM管道越来越多地将OOXML文档作为财务、合规和检索增强工作流中的一等证据消费，隐含假设模型消费的证据与Microsoft Office编辑画布中显示的内容一致。然而作者发现这一假设可能失败：同一文件在Office和LLM提取管道中可产生不同视图，每种视图对其消费者而言都被视为权威，形成"多重真相"（plural ground truth），且摄入契约通常未明确说明哪种视图和语义角色会成为模型证据。这种分歧可能导致模型基于非用户可见的内容做出决策，构成安全隐患。

Method: 作者系统性遍历和挖掘OOXML规范，识别出导致Office视图与LLM提取视图分叉的规范合法的OOXML构造（即"evidence forks"）。他们构建了一个包含13种提取工具的提取面板和4个原生LLM API及7个网络聊天机器人的测试集，每个测试文档携带一个"陷阱"：一个任务相关的事实，仅被提取管道暴露但不在Office中显示。此外还对16个流行开源LLM项目进行源码级调查，分析默认OOXML摄入路径。

Result: 确认了21种跨越Excel、Word和PowerPoint的证据分叉，覆盖六个视图构建维度。13种提取工具中全部至少从一种分叉中发出证据。在21机制评估中，4个API在48-76%的试验中返回陷阱；21种机制中有20种至少有一个界面返回陷阱。测量显示暴露由模型上游的摄入路径和提取器配置决定。开源项目调查表明默认OOXML摄入路径集中在受影响提取器家族上。

Conclusion: OOXML到LLM的管道存在系统性的语义完整性风险：用户所见与模型所见可能不一致，且这种不一致是规范合法的、可被利用的。问题根源在模型上游的摄入路径和提取器配置，而非模型本身。这呼吁对OOXML证据摄入的契约设计、视图选择和推导过程保存提出新的要求，以确保LLM消费的证据与用户认知保持一致。

Abstract: LLM pipelines increasingly ingest Office Open XML (OOXML) documents (Word, Excel, and PowerPoint files) as first-class evidence in financial, compliance, and retrieval-augmented workflows, implicitly assuming semantic integrity: that the evidence consumed by the model matches the content shown in the Microsoft Office suite editing canvas. We show that this assumption can fail in OOXML-to-LLM pipelines. The same specification-valid OOXML file can yield one evidentiary view in Microsoft Office and another when extracted for an LLM. Each view is treated as authoritative by its consumer, a condition we call plural ground truth. The ingestion contract rarely states which view and semantic roles become model evidence or preserves how that evidence was derived. We call the specification-grounded OOXML constructions that induce such divergence evidence forks.
  We systematically traverse and mine the OOXML specification and confirm 21 evidence forks across Excel, Word, and PowerPoint, spanning six dimensions of view construction. All 13 tools in our extraction panel emit evidence from at least one fork. We test four native-ingestion LLM APIs and seven web chatbots. Each test document carries a trap: a task-relevant fact exposed by extraction but not shown in Office. Across this 21-mechanism evaluation, the four APIs return the trap in 48--76% of trials. For 20 of 21 mechanisms, at least one of the eleven interfaces returns the trap. Our measurements further show that exposure is shaped upstream of the model by the ingestion path and extractor configuration. A source-level survey of sixteen popular open-source LLM projects further shows that default OOXML ingestion paths concentrate on affected extractor families.

</details>


### [354] [Answer Is Cheap, Show Me the Evidence! Augmenting Automated Vulnerability Assessment with Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25905)
*Shengyi Pan,Zelong Zheng,Jiayuan Zhou,Xing Hu,Xin Xia,Shanping Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出EAVA框架，利用大语言模型（LLMs）进行软件漏洞（SV）评估，不仅预测评估结果，还提供支持性证据。EAVA通过专门的LLM智能体处理富文本内容和项目信息，并采用两阶段训练流程（监督指令微调+强化学习）构建专用评估模型，同时检索相似历史漏洞作为补充证据。实验表明EAVA在多个指标上超越最强基线5.3%-35.2%，消融实验和用户研究验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化SV评估方法往往忽略SVR中的富文本信息（如截图、代码片段）和漏洞项目的上下文信息，且只关注预测准确性而不提供解释或支持证据，限制了分析人员在实际工作中验证不完美预测的能力。

Method: EAVA框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 专门的LLM智能体处理富文本内容和项目信息；(2) 两阶段训练流程构建专用评估模型——先通过自动标注的推理轨迹进行监督指令微调注入领域知识，再应用强化学习改进内在推理能力；(3) 检索相似历史漏洞作为补充证据。

Result: 在新收集的SVR数据集上，EAVA相比最强基线在多个指标上提升5.3%至35.2%；消融实验确认了评估专用模型训练和信息增强的有效性；与安全专家的用户研究进一步证明EAVA提供的证据在真实世界SV评估中有用且实用。

Conclusion: EAVA通过结合LLM智能体、两阶段训练和证据检索，不仅提升了SV评估的准确性，还提供了可解释的支持性证据，增强了自动化工具在实际安全分析场景中的可用性和可信度。

Abstract: Software vulnerability (SV) assessment helps prioritize remediation by characterizing reported vulnerabilities. Existing
  automated methods predict assessment results from SV reports (SVRs), but often overlook information in rich text, such as
  screenshots and code snippets, as well as contextual information about vulnerable projects. They also focus on prediction
  accuracy without providing explanations or supporting evidence, limiting their practical use when analysts must validate
  imperfect predictions. We propose EAVA, a framework that uses large language models (LLMs) to assess SVs and provide
  supporting evidence. EAVA employs specialized LLM agents to process rich-text content and project information, and builds a
  dedicated assessment model through a two-stage training pipeline. It first uses supervised instruction tuning on automatically
  annotated reasoning trajectories to inject domain knowledge, and then applies reinforcement learning to improve intrinsic
  reasoning. EAVA also retrieves similar historical vulnerabilities as supplementary evidence. Experiments on a newly collected
  SVR dataset show that EAVA outperforms the strongest baseline by 5.3 to 35.2 percent across multiple metrics. Ablation studies
  confirm the effectiveness of assessment-specific model training and information enrichment. A user study with security experts
  further demonstrates that the evidence provided by EAVA is useful and practical for real-world SV assessment.

</details>


### [355] [XREPOTEST: Benchmarking Multilingual Repository-Level Unit Test Generation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25939)
*Dung Le Quang,Dong Cao Van,Nam Le Hai,Linh Ngo Van,Anh M. T. Bui,Phuong T. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了XREPOTEST，一个多语言仓库级单元测试生成基准，涵盖Rust、Go、Julia、PHP和Ruby五种语言，通过容器化执行框架和多种上下文增强策略进行评估，并提出Invocation Rate指标来衡量测试是否真正执行了目标功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM单元测试生成的评估大多基于独立（standalone）设置，且覆盖的编程语言范围狭窄，导致高估了模型在真实场景中的能力。需要更接近真实软件环境的基准来准确评估。

Method: 构建多语言仓库级基准XREPOTEST；使用容器化执行框架模拟真实仓库约束；采用文件级、基于LSP和基于检索的多种上下文增强策略；提出Invocation Rate (IR)指标评估测试是否真正调用目标功能；对14个SOTA LLM进行系统实验。

Result: 实验揭示了独立设置与仓库级性能之间的显著差距，以及更丰富上下文与测试可靠性之间的权衡。XREPOTEST是一个具有挑战性的基准。

Conclusion: XREPOTEST为推动真实软件环境中可扩展、稳健的单元测试生成提供了有信息量的基准，有助于缩小LLM测试生成从研究到实际应用的差距。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for automated unit test generation, but existing evaluations largely rely on standalone settings and a narrow set of programming languages, overestimating real-world readiness. We introduce XREPOTEST, a multilingual repository-level benchmark for unit test generation spanning five underexplored languages: Rust, Go, Julia, PHP, and Ruby. XREPOTEST evaluates tests under realistic repository constraints using a containerized execution framework and multiple context augmentation strategies, including file-level, LSP-based, and retrieval-based context. Beyond standard metrics such as test pass rate and coverage, we propose Invocation Rate (IR) to assess whether generated tests meaningfully exercise the intended functionality. Experiments with 14 state-of-the-art LLMs, including Claude 4.5, GPT-5.2, DeepSeek V4-Pro, and Qwen families, reveal a substantial gap between standalone and repository-level performance, as well as trade-offs between richer context and test reliability. Overall, XREPOTEST provides a challenging and informative benchmark to advance scalable and robust unit test generation in realistic software environments. The dataset and code are publicly available at: https://github.com/solis-team/XRepoTest

</details>


### [356] [Vulnerable Code Search: Transferable Attack for Code Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26031)
*Kaicheng Wang,Liyan Huang,Jesse Thomason,Weihang Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种编程语言无关、可迁移的对抗性攻击方法，通过扰动代码中的标识符（不改变功能）使任意不相关代码与目标查询对齐，从而破坏神经代码检索系统的性能，在多个SOTA模型上将MRR指标降低高达77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于神经代码语言模型（CLM）的代码检索工具容易受到针对非功能性文本元素的对抗性攻击，这暴露了现有代码搜索方法的脆弱性，需要被重视和研究。

Method: 提出一种编程语言无关、可迁移的对抗性攻击方法：扰动代码片段中的标识符（如变量名、函数名等），在不改变代码功能的前提下，使代码与目标查询在嵌入空间中人工对齐。攻击在小模型（如CodeT5+）上计算，可迁移到更大的闭源嵌入模型（如Voyage-code-3）或LLM（如Gemini-3.1-Pro）。

Result: 攻击方法能显著提高查询与任意不相关代码片段之间的相似度，将SOTA模型的MRR（平均倒数排名）指标最多降低77%，且在小模型上计算的攻击能有效迁移到大型闭源模型。

Conclusion: 实验结果凸显了当前代码搜索方法对对抗性攻击的脆弱性，强调了开发更健壮、语义感知的代码检索方法的必要性。

Abstract: Reliable code retrieval is crucial for developer productivity and effective code reuse. However, current neural code language models (CLMs) powering search tools are susceptible to adversarial attacks targeting non-functional textual elements. In this paper, we introduce a programming language-agnostic, transferable, adversarial attack that exploits this CLM vulnerability. Our approach perturbs identifiers within a code snippet without altering the snippet's functionality to artificially align the code with a target query. We demonstrate that our attack, even when computed using smaller code embedding models, such as CodeT5+, is highly effective and transferable to larger, closed-source embedding models, like Voyage-code-3, or LLMs like Gemini-3.1-Pro. Our attack can increase the similarity between the query and arbitrary, irrelevant code snippets, consequently degrading key retrieval metrics such as the Mean Reciprocal Rank (MRR) of state-of-the-art models by up to 77%. The experimental results highlight the fragility of current code search methods and underscore the need for more robust, semantic-aware approaches.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [357] [Sample Complexity of the Second-Best Bilateral Trade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25303)
*Qiaoyun Shi,Shengxin Liu,Zongqi Wan*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究学习近最优双边贸易机制的样本复杂度，关注满足贝叶斯激励相容（BIC）、事后个人理性（IIR）和事前弱预算平衡（WBB）的机制，目标是达到次优贸易收益（SB）基准。作者在三个体制下给出了匹配或接近匹配的上下界：正则乘积分布下的加性ε近似样本复杂度为Θ̃(h²/ε²)；乘性(1-α)近似下为Θ̃(h/(SB(D)α²))，依赖基准SB(D)；无界分布（MHR假设）下样本复杂度取决于比值χ_μ(D)=μ(D)/SB(D)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于双边贸易机制学习的工作多聚焦于简单或固定价格机制，而本文首次系统地研究学习满足BIC、IIR和WBB的通用机制，以逼近次优贸易收益这一黄金基准，填补了样本复杂度分析在该领域的空白。

Method: 作者在三种体制下分别推导样本复杂度的上下界：1）有界区间[0,h]²上的正则乘积分布，分析加性ε逼近；2）同样假设下的乘性(1-α)逼近，发现复杂度对基准SB(D)有不可避免的依赖；3）无界分布加上单调风险率（MHR）假设，复杂度由比值χ_μ(D)=μ(D)/SB(D)刻画。上界通过构造样本化机制实现，下界通过信息论或统计论证建立。

Result: 给出了三个体制下匹配或接近匹配的上下界：加性近似为Θ̃(h²/ε²)，乘性近似为Θ̃(h/(SB(D)α²))（基准敏感），无界MHR情形下依赖于χ_μ(D)=μ(D)/SB(D)。

Conclusion: 研究表明，学习BIC/IIR/WBB双边贸易机制的样本复杂度在不同近似准则和分布假设下呈现出不同的量级，其中乘性逼近对基准SB(D)的依赖以及无界分布下对比值χ_μ(D)的依赖揭示了该问题的内在统计难度，为实际机制设计提供了样本复杂度指引。

Abstract: We study the sample complexity of learning near-optimal bilateral trade mechanisms. Unlike previous work on learning simple or fixed-price bilateral-trade mechanisms, we focus on mechanisms satisfying Bayesian incentive compatibility (BIC), interim individual rationality (IIR), and ex-ante weak budget balance (WBB). In other words, our target is to design a sample-based mechanism that achieves the second-best gains-from-trade benchmark.
  We give matching or nearly matching upper and lower bounds in three regimes. For regular product distributions on $[0,h]^2$, additive $\varepsilon$-approximation has sample complexity $\widetildeΘ(h^2/\varepsilon^2)$. For multiplicative $(1-α)$-approximation under the same assumptions, we find that the sample complexity is $\widetildeΘ(h/(\mathrm{SB}(D)α^2))$, which is benchmark-sensitive with unavoidable dependence on the second-best gains from trade $\mathrm{SB}(D)$. We also investigate unbounded distributions under a monotone hazard rate (MHR) assumption. The sample complexity depends on the ratio $χ_μ(D)=μ(D)/\mathrm{SB}(D)$, where $μ(D)$ is the sum of the buyer's expected value and the seller's expected cost.

</details>
